~~~python
    return Model(['x'], [1], [1], lambda s, u: ({(1,): eps, (0,): 1 - eps} if (u == 'w' and s[0] == 0) else {s: 1.0}), (0,), ['w', 'rest'])
def slow_leak(p=0.02):
    def step(s, u):
        if u == 'w': return {(1,): 1.0}
        if s[0] == 1: return {(1,): 1 - p, (0,): p}
        return {s: 1.0}
    return Model(['charge'], [1], [1], step, (0,), ['w', 'rest'], cut=lambda st: step(st, 'rest'))  # 漏失与供给无关
def long_prep(n=6):  # 须连续 n 次预备 a；静置或过早写入使计数清零
    def step(s, u):
        c, x = s
        if u == 'a': c = min(n, c + 1)
        elif u == 'w' and c >= n: x = 1
        else: c = 0
        return {(c, x): 1.0}
    return Model(['prep', 'x'], [0, 1], [1, 1], step, (0, 0), ['a', 'w', 'rest'], cut=lambda st: {(0, st[1]): 1.0})
def fuse_rom():
    return Model(['bit', 'lock'], [1, 1], [1, 1], lambda s, u: {((1, 1) if (u == 'w' and not s[1]) else s): 1.0}, (0, 0), ['w', 'p', 'rest'],
                 act=lambda s: 0, cut=lambda st: {st: 1.0})
def refresh_dram():  # 刷新（能量供给）维持电荷；撤供即失
    return Model(['charge'], [1], [1], lambda s, u: {((1,) if u == 'w' else s): 1.0}, (0,), ['w', 'rest'], act=lambda s: s[0],
                 cut=lambda s: {(0,): 1.0})
def receiver_saturation():
    K = 3
    def step(s, u):
        x, y = s
        if u == 'w': y = clip(y + 1, 0, 2)
        if u == 'a': x = clip(x + 1, 0, K)
        if u == 'p' and y > 0 and x < K: x = x + 1
        return {(x, y): 1.0}
    return Model(['x', 'y'], [1, 1], [1, 1], step, (0, 0), ['w', 'a', 'p', 'rest'], lambda s: s[0])
def spontaneous_channel():  # 槽深 y（申报块）塑造静置时的自发流量 o；来水 p 时流量 2y+1
    def step(s, u):
        y, o = s
        if u == 'w': y = clip(y + 1, 0, 2)
        o = 2 * y + 1 if u == 'p' else y
        return {(y, o): 1.0}
    return Model(['depth', 'flow'], [1, 0], [1, 1], step, (0, 0), ['w', 'p', 'rest'], act=lambda s: s[1],
                 cut=lambda st: {(st[0], 0): 1.0})
def recording(M, seen):  # 包装模型：记录判定过程中实际施加过的每一输入符号
    def step(st, u):
        seen.add(u); return M.step(st, u)
    return Model(M.names, [1 if i in M.MED else 0 for i in range(len(M.names))], [1 if i in M.IN else 0 for i in range(len(M.names))],
                 step, M.init, M.alpha, M.act, M.cut)
def dep_unsampled():  # Dep 的有限取样：w 使 z = 1；静置时 Δx = 1 当且仅当 z = 1 且 y ≥ 3，在水平 {0, 1, 2} 上处处无差异
    def step(s, u):
        x, y, z = s
        if u == 'w': return {(x, y, 1): 1.0}
        return {(x + (1 if (z == 1 and y >= 3) else 0), y, z): 1.0}
    return Model(['x', 'y', 'z'], [1, 1, 1], [1, 1, 1], step, (0, 0, 0), ['w', 'rest'])
def selfread():  # act 即留存状态本身；写入饱和。Act_𝓟 = 0
    return Model(['y'], [1], [1], lambda s, u: {((min(2, s[0] + 1),) if u == 'w' else s): 1.0}, (0,), ['w', 'rest'], act=lambda s: s[0])
def usage_ltp():  # 读出即增强：探测 p 时输出 o = y+1 且 y 加一；静置时 o = 0
    def step(s, u):
        y, o = s
        if u == 'w': return {(min(3, y + 1), 0): 1.0}
        if u == 'p': return {(min(3, y + 1), y + 1): 1.0}
        return {(y, 0): 1.0}
    return Model(['y', 'o'], [1, 0], [1, 1], step, (0, 0), ['w', 'p', 'rest'], act=lambda s: s[1])
def linear_additive():
    return Model(['x', 'y'], [1, 1], [1, 1], lambda s, u: {(clip(s[0] + s[1], -30, 30), clip(s[1] + (1 if u == 'w' else 0), 0, 2)): 1.0}, (0, 0), ['w', 'rest'])
def tests():
    T = []
    add = lambda name, got, exp: T.append((name, got, exp, '✓' if got == exp else '✗'))
    add('回声 Tr（精确）', det_tr_exact(echo())[0], 0)
    # 引理一（度量）：相关态
    M = correlated_bits()
    add('相关比特 联合TV Tr', det_tr_enum(M, q=2, k=1, H=2)[0], 1)
    marg = max(tv(push(d1, lambda s: s[i]), push(d2, lambda s: s[i])) for i in (0, 1)
               for d1, d2 in [run_pair(M, ['eq'], ['ne'])])
    add('相关比特 单变量边缘最大差异 > r？', int(marg > 0.05), 0)
    f = lambda s: (s[0], s[0] ^ s[1])
    add('相关比特 分块重编码后 Tr', det_tr_enum(recode(M, f, f, [1, 1], [1, 1]), q=2, k=1, H=2)[0], 1)
    add('守恒成对 Tr', det_tr_exact(conservative_pair())[0], 1)
    add('镜像存储 Tr', det_tr_exact(mirror())[0], 1)
    # 引理二
    M = mutual_external()
    add('互相回写外存 不钳定 Tr', det_tr_enum(M, q=4, k=1, H=2, clamp=False)[0], 1)
    add('互相回写外存 钳定 Tr（精确）', det_tr_exact(M, q=4, k=1)[0], 0)
    add('互相回写外存 Act_𝓟', det_act(M, q=2, H=2, P=1, probes=probe_class(M, 1)), 0)
    # 随机钳定：转移核的支持域与孪生协议
    add('随机界外（只给转移核）Tr → ⊥_Obs', det_tr_enum(noisy_external(), q=2, k=1, H=2)[0], OBS)
    add('随机界外（申报结构表示）孪生协议下可判：差异只经界外传递，钳定后消去，Tr', det_tr_enum(noisy_external_struct(), q=2, k=1, H=2)[0], 0)
    M = null_signal_struct()
    a, b = run_twin(M, ['w', 'rest', 'rest'], ['w', 'rest', 'rest'])
    a2, b2 = run_twin(M, ['w', 'rest', 'rest'], ['w', 'rest', 'rest'], shared=False)
    yb = lambda d: push(d, lambda st: st[2])
    # 两个数学对象分列：边缘分布的 TV 与成对联合分布上的不一致概率 Pr(y₁ ≠ y₂)
    def dis(J): return round(float(sum(p for (s1, s2), p in J.items() if s1[2] != s2[2])), 4)
    hh = ['w', 'rest', 'rest']
    add('信号不入系统 同史、共享扰动：y 的边缘 TV / Pr(y₁ ≠ y₂)（解析：0 / 0）',
        (round(float(tv(yb(a), yb(b))), 4), dis(twin_joint(M, hh, hh))), (0.0, 0.0))
    add('信号不入系统 同史、独立抽样：y 的边缘 TV / Pr(y₁ ≠ y₂)（解析：0.5 / 0.5；基线侧 y ≡ 0，对照侧 y = m_对 ⊕ m_基 均匀）',
        (round(float(tv(yb(a2), yb(b2))), 4), dis(twin_joint(M, hh, hh, shared=False))), (0.5, 0.5))
    a3, b3 = run_twin(M, ['w', 'rest', 'rest'], ['rest', 'rest', 'rest'])
    add('信号不入系统 异史、共享扰动：y 的边缘 TV / Pr(y₁ ≠ y₂)（解析：0 / 0）',
        (round(float(tv(yb(a3), yb(b3))), 4), dis(twin_joint(M, ['w', 'rest', 'rest'], ['rest', 'rest', 'rest']))), (0.0, 0.0))
    Mn = null_signal_noext()
    j1 = twin_joint(Mn, hh, hh, shared=False); m1_, m2_ = push(j1, lambda ab: ab[0][0]), push(j1, lambda ab: ab[1][0])
    add('无界外分量（不钳定）同史、独立抽样：边缘 TV / Pr(两侧不一致)（解析：0 / 0.5——无覆写时边缘与耦合无关）',
        (round(float(tv(m1_, m2_)), 4), round(float(sum(p for (s1, s2), p in j1.items() if s1[0] != s2[0])), 4)), (0.0, 0.5))
    add('信号不入系统：孪生协议下 Tr', det_tr_enum(M, q=3, k=1, H=2)[0], 0)
    M = hybrid_struct(3)
    w1 = det_tr_enum(M, q=2, k=1, H=4); w2 = det_tr_enum(hybrid(3), q=2, k=1, H=4)
    add('钳定混合态（C = 3，结构表示）：孪生协议与值钳定的判词与见证一致', (w1[0], w1[1] == w2[1]), (1, True))
    # 分辨阈
    add('分辨阈合法域：镜像存储 r = 0.05 判 1；r = 1、r = −0.1 越出 Dom2 判 ⊥_Dom（枚举与精确判定）',
        (det_tr_exact(mirror(), r=0.05)[0], det_tr_exact(mirror(), r=1)[0], det_tr_enum(mirror(), r=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_exact(mirror(), r=-0.1)[0]), (1, DOM, DOM, DOM))
    add('分辨阈 r = 0 合法：镜像存储判 1', det_tr_exact(mirror(), r=0)[0], 1)
    # 合法历史集合由监控自动机申报（定理一 (c)）
    M = gate_once()
    add('全序列语言有见证；𝓗 禁止 w 时无见证（精确判定与枚举）',
        (det_tr_exact(M, q=1)[0], det_tr_exact(M, q=1, mon=MON_NO_W)[0], det_tr_enum(M, q=1, H=3, mon=MON_NO_W)[0]), (1, 0, 0))
    g1 = det_tr_exact(M, q=1); g2 = det_tr_exact(M, q=1, mon=MON_W_AFTER_A); g3 = det_tr_enum(M, q=1, H=3, mon=MON_W_AFTER_A)
    add('𝓗 要求 w 在 a 之后：全序列最短见证史长 1 被禁止，合法最短见证史长 2，枚举见证合法',
        (g1[1], g2[0], g2[1], g3[0], mon_accepts(MON_W_AFTER_A, g3[1][0]) and mon_accepts(MON_W_AFTER_A, g3[1][1])), (1, 1, 2, 1, True))
    # 定理一
    g, L, N, I, B, _ = det_tr_exact(hybrid(), q=2, k=1)
    add(f'钳定混合态 精确 Tr（L={L}，N={N}，N²(k+1)={N*N*2}，|I′|={I}，界={B}）', (g, L <= B, L > N * N * 2), (1, True, True))
    for rd in ('撤', '静'):
        M = long_prep(6)
        add(f'长预备史 枚举 H=6 Tr^{rd}（界内 0：只对 H_max=6 成立）', det_tr_enum(M, q=1, k=1, H=6, reading=rd)[0], 0)
        g, L, N, I, B, _ = det_tr_exact(M, q=1, k=1, reading=rd)
        add(f'长预备史 精确 Tr^{rd}（全图搜索，L={L}，N={N}，界={B}；与界内 0 分别记录）', (g, L), (1, 7))
    # 定理二与有界外反例
    add('门控时钟 k=3 个位置（H=9）', det_tr_enum(gated_clock(), q=1, k=3, H=9)[0], 1)
    add('随机亚阈 k=1（r=0.08）', det_tr_enum(prob_write(.05), q=1, r=.08, k=1, H=3)[0], 0)
    add('随机亚阈 k=2（r=0.08）', det_tr_enum(prob_write(.05), q=1, r=.08, k=2, H=3)[0], 1)
    # 定理四：第一层判词是 (Prof, C_op) 的泛函。同一 Prof（同一随机写入转移核），只改 C_op 的 r 或 k，判词翻转
    Mp = prob_write(.05)
    add('同一 Prof、改 C_op 的 r（H_max=1，k=1）：r = 0.01 判 1，r = 0.08 判 0',
        (det_tr_enum(Mp, q=1, r=.01, k=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_enum(Mp, q=1, r=.08, k=1, H=1)[0]), (1, 0))
    add('同一 Prof、改 C_op 的 k（H_max=2，r=0.08）：k = 1 判 0，k = 2 判 1（(w,w) 对 (rest,rest)，TV = 0.0975）',
        (det_tr_enum(Mp, q=1, r=.08, k=1, H=2)[0], det_tr_enum(Mp, q=1, r=.08, k=2, H=2)[0]), (0, 1))
    add('同一 Prof、同一 C_op：判词相同（两次独立构造的模型）',
        det_tr_enum(prob_write(.05), q=1, r=.08, k=2, H=2)[0] == det_tr_enum(Mp, q=1, r=.08, k=2, H=2)[0], True)
    M = table_model()
    add('有界外反例 钳定 k=1', det_tr_exact(M, q=1, k=1)[0], 0)
    add('有界外反例 钳定 k=2', det_tr_exact(M, q=1, k=2)[0], 1)
    add('有界外模型 不钳定 k=2（与定理二 (a) 一致：k=1、2 皆无见证）', (det_tr_enum(M, q=1, k=1, H=4, clamp=False)[0], det_tr_enum(M, q=1, k=2, H=4, clamp=False)[0]), (0, 0))
    # 引理五
    add('介质漏失 q=6 Tr^静', det_tr_enum(slow_leak(), q=6, k=1, H=1)[0], 1)
    add('介质漏失 q=300 Tr^静', det_tr_enum(slow_leak(), q=300, k=1, H=1)[0], 0)
    add('介质漏失 q=6 Tr^撤（申报撤供律）', det_tr_enum(slow_leak(), q=6, k=1, H=1, reading='撤')[0], 1)
    add('介质漏失 q=300 Tr^撤', det_tr_enum(slow_leak(), q=300, k=1, H=1, reading='撤')[0], 0)
    add('未申报撤供 Tr^撤 → ⊥_Undef', det_tr_exact(mirror(), reading='撤')[0], UNDEF)
    # 撤供检验
    M = refresh_dram()
    add('刷新维持电荷 Tr^静', det_tr_exact(M, q=2, reading='静')[0], 1)
    add('刷新维持电荷 Tr^撤', det_tr_exact(M, q=2, reading='撤')[0], 0)
    # 熔丝 ROM：起点与记录
    M = fuse_rom()
    add('熔丝ROM 自空白起有记录 Hist^撤/Hist^静', (det_hist(M, ['w'], {0}, q=2, reading='撤', init=(0, 0)),
                                             det_hist(M, ['w'], {0}, q=2, reading='静', init=(0, 0))), (1, 1))
    add('熔丝ROM 自已烧写态起无记录 Hist^静', det_hist(M, None, set(), q=2, init=(1, 1)), OBS)
    # Act：自发活动
    M = spontaneous_channel()
    base = run_pair(M, ['w', 'rest'], ['rest', 'rest'])
    add('自发流道 探测前 act 已不同', int(tv(push(base[0], M.act), push(base[1], M.act)) > .05), 1)
    add('自发流道 Act_𝓟（(b) 款增量判据）', det_act(M, q=1, H=1, P=1, probes=probe_class(M, 1)), 1)
    add('受者饱和 Act_𝓟', det_act(receiver_saturation(), q=1, H=2, P=1, probes=probe_class(receiver_saturation(), 1)), 1)
    add('自读饱和（act 即留存状态，探测为写入）Act_𝓟', det_act(selfread(), q=1, k=2, H=2, P=1, probes=probe_class(selfread(), 1)), 0)
    add('读出即增强 Act_𝓟（经 (a) 款）', det_act(usage_ltp(), q=1, H=1, P=1, probes=probe_class(usage_ltp(), 1)), 1)
    # Dep
    add('受者饱和 Dep（读于 p）', det_dep(receiver_saturation(), ['a', 'a', 'a'], {0, 1, 2}, 0, 1, [0, 1], u='p'), 1)
    M = receiver_saturation()
    res = run_pair(M, ['a', 'a', 'a'], ['rest', 'rest', 'rest'])
    s1, s2 = _point(res[0]), _point(res[1])
    e1, e2 = y_effect(M, s1, 0, 1, 0, 1, 'p'), y_effect(M, s2, 0, 1, 0, 1, 'p')
    dmu, drho = abs(e1[0] - e2[0]), abs(e1[1] - e2[1])
    add(f'受者饱和 两项效应差（|Δμ 差| = {dmu}，|ρ 差| = {drho}）', (dmu > 0, drho >= 0), (True, True))
    add('Dep 双阈：r_μ 恰在 |Δμ 差| 之上、r_ρ 在 |ρ 差| 之上 → 0；r_μ 降到其下 → 1',
        (det_dep(M, ['a', 'a', 'a'], {0, 1, 2}, 0, 1, [0, 1], r_mu=dmu, r_rho=drho + 1, u='p'),
         det_dep(M, ['a', 'a', 'a'], {0, 1, 2}, 0, 1, [0, 1], r_mu=dmu / 2, r_rho=drho + 1, u='p')), (0, 1))
    M = shape_model()     # z = 0：Δμ = 1、ρ = 0；z = 1：Δμ = 2、ρ = 1；两项差均为 1
    add('Dep 双阈（两项差分处阈值两侧）：(r_μ, r_ρ) = (2, 0.5)、(2, 1.5)、(0.5, 1.5)',
        tuple(det_dep(M, ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [0, 1], r_mu=a_, r_rho=b_, u='p') for a_, b_ in [(2, .5), (2, 1.5), (.5, 1.5)]), (1, 0, 1))
    add('Dep 阈值域：r_μ < 0 判 ⊥_Dom', det_dep(M, ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [0, 1], r_mu=-1, u='p'), DOM)
    M = spontaneous_channel()     # 探测前 act 已不同，(a) 款不成立，判词只由 (b) 款给出
    add('Act_𝓟 (b) 款的数值增量阈 r_act 单列：自发流道 r_act = 0.5 判 1、r_act = 5 判 0',
        (det_act(M, q=1, H=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=probe_class(M, 1)), det_act(M, q=1, H=1, P=1, r_act=5, probes=probe_class(M, 1))), (1, 0))
    # 探测类 𝓟 是冻结参数：判定器只遍历申报的探测，不缺省为全字母表（3.1（六），4.3）
    M = spontaneous_channel()
    add('Act_𝓟 随申报的探测类改变（q=1，r_act=0.5，史 w 对 rest）：𝓟 = {rest} 判 0，𝓟 = {p} 判 1，𝓟 = 全部长度 1 探测 判 1',
        tuple(det_act_h(M, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=ps) for ps in ([('rest',)], [('p',)], probe_class(M, 1))), (0, 1, 1))
    add('Act_𝓟 前瞻式同样随 𝓟 改变：𝓟 = {rest} 判 0，𝓟 = {p} 判 1',
        (det_act(M, q=1, H=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('rest',)]), det_act(M, q=1, H=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('p',)])), (0, 1))
    add('𝓟 未申报判 ⊥_Undef；空集合判 0；越出长度界或字母表判 ⊥_Dom；探测越出合法历史集合判 ⊥_Dom',
        (det_act_h(M, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5), det_act_h(M, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[]),
         det_act_h(M, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('p', 'p')]), det_act_h(M, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('x',)]),
         det_act(M, q=1, H=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('p',)], mon=(0, {(0, 'w'): 0, (0, 'rest'): 0}, {0}))),
        (UNDEF, 0, DOM, DOM, DOM))
    seen = set(); Mr = recording(spontaneous_channel(), seen)
    det_act_h(Mr, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('rest',)])
    add('𝓟 = {rest} 时判定过程实际施加的输入只有史与静置中的 w、rest，不调用 𝓟 外的 p', sorted(seen), ['rest', 'w'])
    # Dep 的设定域：有限离散水平集须完全枚举；无限设定域只给界内 0
    M = dep_unsampled()
    Vnat = ('infinite', lambda a: isinstance(a, int) and a >= 0)      # V_y = 非负整数（无限），成员判定可执行
    add('Dep 设定域：V_y 无限、只测 {0,1,2} → 界内 0 并报告已测子域（不签全域 0）；测入 3 → 1；有限 V_y 与取样不符 → ⊥_Dom；完全枚举有限 V_y={0,1,2} → 0',
        (det_dep(M, ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [0, 1, 2], domain=Vnat), det_dep(M, ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [0, 1, 2, 3], domain=Vnat),
         det_dep(M, ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [0, 1, 2], domain=[0, 1, 2, 3]), det_dep(M, ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [0, 1, 2])),
        ((IN0, (0, 1, 2)), 1, DOM, 0))
    add('Dep 无限域的取样合法性：已测水平 −1 不属 V_y → ⊥_Dom；只写「无限」而无成员判定 → ⊥_Dom',
        (det_dep(M, ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [-1, 0, 1], domain=Vnat), det_dep(M, ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [0, 1, 2], domain='infinite')), (DOM, DOM))
    # 转移核的合法性：概率非负、和为 1；精确判定另要求唯一正概率后继
    bad = {'和 0.8': lambda st, u: {st: 0.8}, '负概率': lambda st, u: {st: 1.2, (1 - st[0],): -0.2},
           '空分布': lambda st, u: {}, '单点 0.3': lambda st, u: {((1,) if u == 'w' else st): 0.3}}
    add('不合法转移核（和 0.8、负概率、空分布、单点概率 0.3）：精确判定与枚举都判 ⊥_Dom，不冒充合法确定模型',
        tuple((det_tr_exact(Model(['x'], [1], [1], f, (0,), ['w', 'rest']), q=1)[0],
               det_tr_enum(Model(['x'], [1], [1], f, (0,), ['w', 'rest']), q=1, H=1)[0]) for f in bad.values()),
        ((DOM, DOM),) * 4)
    add('扰动分布不合法（和 0.9）：孪生协议判 ⊥_Dom',
        det_tr_enum(Model(['x'], [1], [1], None, (0,), ['w', 'rest'], struct=(lambda st, u, w: (w,), {0: .5, 1: .4})), q=1, H=1)[0], DOM)
    add('零概率项不改变确定性：{后继: 1, 另一: 0} 按确定转移判定',
        det_tr_exact(Model(['x'], [1], [1], lambda st, u: {((1,) if u == 'w' else st): 1.0, (7,): 0.0}, (0,), ['w', 'rest']), q=1)[0], 1)
    # 跨拍扰动：各拍 ω_t 独立同分布（两侧同拍共享）；跨拍相关须作为扩张状态申报（3.3）
    def iid_noise():          # x ← x ⊕ ω_t（只在 w 拍），ω_t 逐拍独立的公平硬币
        return Model(['x'], [1], [1], None, (0,), ['w', 'rest'], struct=(lambda st, u, w: ((st[0] ^ w) if u == 'w' else st[0],), {0: .5, 1: .5}))
    def persistent_noise():   # 同一单拍边缘，跨拍完全相关：界外扩张分量 c 首拍取 ω、此后保持（2 表示未取）；x ← x ⊕ c（只在 w 拍）
        def phi(st, u, w):
            x, c = st; cc = w if c == 2 else c
            return ((x ^ cc) if u == 'w' else x, cc)
        return Model(['x', 'c'], [1, 0], [1, 0], None, (0, 2), ['w', 'rest'], struct=(phi, {0: .5, 1: .5}))
    def tvx(M):
        a, b = run_twin(M, ['w', 'w', 'rest'], ['rest', 'w', 'rest'])
        return round(float(tv(push(a, lambda st: st[0]), push(b, lambda st: st[0]))), 4)
    add('跨拍独立与跨拍相关（单拍边缘相同）不合并：史对 (w,w) 对 (rest,w) 的 x 边缘 TV 分别为 0 与 0.5',
        (tvx(iid_noise()), tvx(persistent_noise())), (0.0, 0.5))
    # 结构表示与撤供（3.3）：结构表示须在同一扰动空间上申报 cut_phi；只给撤供核者为不完整申报，撤供读法判 ⊥_Undef
    def struct_cut(with_phi):
        phi = lambda st, u, w: ((1,) if u == 'w' else st)
        cut_k = lambda st: {(0,): 1.0}
        return Model(['x'], [1], [1], None, (0,), ['w', 'rest'], cut=cut_k,
                     struct=(phi, {0: 1.0}, (lambda st, w: (0,))) if with_phi else (phi, {0: 1.0}))
    add('结构表示 + 单独撤供核（缺 cut_phi）：Tr^撤、Hist^撤 判 ⊥_Undef，Tr^静 照常；补 cut_phi 后 Tr^撤 有合法判值',
        (det_tr_enum(struct_cut(False), q=1, H=1, reading='撤')[0], det_hist(struct_cut(False), ['w'], {0}, q=1, reading='撤'),
         det_tr_enum(struct_cut(False), q=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_enum(struct_cut(True), q=1, H=1, reading='撤')[0]),
        (UNDEF, UNDEF, 1, 0))
    # 历史式 Act_𝓟^h 与合法历史集合（3.1（三））：𝓗 允许原史而禁止探测延伸时判 ⊥_Dom
    M = spontaneous_channel()
    MON_NO_P = (0, {(0, 'w'): 0, (0, 'rest'): 0}, {0})
    add('Act_𝓟^h：𝓗 允许 w、rest 而禁止探测 p → ⊥_Dom（不凭非法探测判 1）；𝓗 不约束时同一 𝓟 判 1；实际史不属 𝓗 → ⊥_Dom',
        (det_act_h(M, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('p',)], mon=MON_NO_P),
         det_act_h(M, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('p',)]),
         det_act_h(M, ['p'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('rest',)], mon=MON_NO_P)), (DOM, 1, DOM))
    # 统一申报入口（4.2）：错误申报参数族
    add('申报入口：q = −1、k = 0、H = 0、r_act < 0、监控自动机格式错 → ⊥_Dom',
        (det_tr_enum(mirror(), q=-1, H=1)[0], det_tr_enum(mirror(), q=1, k=0, H=1)[0], det_tr_enum(mirror(), q=1, H=0)[0],
         det_act(mirror(), q=1, H=1, P=1, r_act=-1, probes=[('rest',)]), det_tr_enum(mirror(), q=1, H=1, mon=('bad',))[0]),
        (DOM,) * 5)
    add('申报入口：位名为空、位名重复、初态长度不符、字母表缺静置 → ⊥_Dom',
        (det_tr_enum(Model([], [], [], lambda st, u: {st: 1.0}, (), ['w', 'rest']), q=1, H=1)[0],
         det_tr_enum(Model(['A', 'A'], [1, 1], [1, 1], lambda st, u: {st: 1.0}, (0, 0), ['w', 'rest']), q=1, H=1)[0],
         det_tr_enum(Model(['A'], [1], [1], lambda st, u: {st: 1.0}, (0, 0), ['w', 'rest']), q=1, H=1)[0],
         det_tr_enum(Model(['A'], [1], [1], lambda st, u: {st: 1.0}, (0,), ['w']), q=1, H=1)[0]),
        (DOM,) * 4)
    # 4.1 五元组报告：每一公开判定器都可写成 Det(v, Code, Level, Dom, Read)
    d1 = report('Tr', mirror(), q=1, H=1); d2 = report('Tr_exact', mutual_external(), q=4)
    d3 = report('Dep', dep_unsampled(), ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [0, 1, 2], domain=Vnat); d4 = report('Tr', mirror(), q=1, H=1, reading='撤')
    d5 = report('Act_h', spontaneous_channel(), ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5); d6 = report('Hist', mirror(), ['w'], {0}, q=1)
    add('五元组报告：镜像存储 Tr（1，见证，Y₁）；互相回写精确 Tr（0，全图搜索）；Dep 无限域（0，界内 0，已测子域）',
        ((d1.v, d1.Code, d1.Level, d1.Read['见证'] is not None), (d2.v, d2.Code.startswith('全图搜索'), d2.Read['最短史长']),
         (d3.v, d3.Code.startswith('界内 0'), d3.Read['已测子域'])), ((1, '见证', 'Y₁', True), (0, True, None), (0, True, (0, 1, 2))))
    add('五元组报告：⊥ 的原因码进入 Code（镜像存储未申报撤供 ⊥_Undef、𝓟 未申报 ⊥_Undef）；Hist 判 1；Dom 含判定模式、耦合协议、𝓗、起点与 C_op',
        ((d4.v, d4.Code), (d5.v, d5.Code), d6.v, sorted(d1.Dom), d1.Dom['C_op']['q']),
        (('⊥', UNDEF), ('⊥', UNDEF), 1, ['C_op', '判定模式', '耦合协议', '起点 s₀', '𝓗'], 1))
    # 五元组 Dom 记录实际生效的配置：位置参数与关键字参数等价；缺省值照记；改动参数则 Dom 随之不同
    ra = report('Tr', mirror(), 2, 0.05, 1, 2); rb = report('Tr', mirror(), q=2, r=0.05, k=1, H=2)
    rd = report('Tr', mirror()); rc = report('Tr', mirror(), q=2, r=0.05, k=1, H=3)
    add('五元组 Dom：位置参数与关键字参数给出相同的 Dom；缺省调用记录实际生效的 q=6、r=0.05、k=1、H=4；改 H 则 Dom 不同',
        (ra.Dom == rb.Dom, (rd.Dom['C_op']['q'], rd.Dom['C_op']['r'], rd.Dom['C_op']['k'], rd.Dom['C_op']['H']), rc.Dom != rb.Dom, rb.Dom['起点 s₀']),
        (True, (6, 0.05, 1, 4), True, (0, 0)))
    M = spontaneous_channel()
    pa = report('Act_h', M, ['w'], {0}, 1, 0.05, 1, probes=[('rest',)], r_act=0.5); pb = report('Act_h', M, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('p',)])
    ma = report('Tr', mirror(), q=1, H=1, mon=MON_NO_W); mb = report('Tr', mirror(), q=1, H=1, mon=MON_W_AFTER_A)
    add('五元组 Dom：探测集合不同则 Dom 不同并记录所申报的 𝓟；监控自动机记冻结摘要，不同 𝓗 摘要不同；Dep 的无限设定域记成员判定',
        (pa.Dom['C_op']['probes'], pb.Dom['C_op']['probes'], pa.Dom != pb.Dom, ma.Dom['𝓗'] != mb.Dom['𝓗'], ma.Dom['𝓗'].startswith('监控自动机'),
         report('Dep', dep_unsampled(), ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [0, 1, 2], domain=Vnat).Dom['C_op']['domain'][0]),
        ((('rest',),), (('p',),), True, True, True, 'infinite'))
    # 探测合法性先于见证检查（3.1（三）（六））：𝓗 禁止 w 时，𝓟 = {w} 判 ⊥_Dom，不被无见证的 0 遮蔽
    add('探测合法性前置：𝓗 禁止 w、𝓟 = {w}：前瞻式与历史式都判 ⊥_Dom；合法空 𝓟 判 0；合法 𝓟 = {rest} 而无见证判 0',
        (det_act(mirror(), q=1, r=.05, k=1, H=2, P=1, probes=[('w',)], mon=MON_NO_W),
         det_act_h(mirror(), ['rest'], [0], q=1, P=1, probes=[('w',)], mon=MON_NO_W),
         det_act(mirror(), q=1, k=1, H=2, P=1, probes=[], mon=MON_NO_W),
         det_act(mirror(), q=1, k=1, H=2, P=1, probes=[('rest',)], mon=MON_NO_W)), (DOM, DOM, 0, 0))
    # 历史段索引：类型、上下界与重复须合法
    add('历史段越界、负索引、非法类型、重复索引 → ⊥_Dom（det_hist、det_act_h、det_dep）；合法段照常判定',
        (det_hist(mirror(), ['w'], [99], q=1), det_act_h(mirror(), ['w'], [99], q=1, probes=[]), det_dep(mirror(), ['w'], [99], 0, 1, [0, 1]),
         det_hist(mirror(), ['w'], [-1], q=1), det_hist(mirror(), ['w'], ['0'], q=1), det_hist(mirror(), ['w', 'w'], [0, 0], q=1),
         det_hist(mirror(), ['w'], [0], q=1)), (DOM,) * 6 + (1,))
    # 每次调用的覆盖参数：起点维度、变量索引、输入字母表
    add('起点维度不符、Dep 变量索引越界、输入不在字母表 → ⊥_Dom，无未处理异常',
        (det_tr_enum(mirror(), init=(1,), q=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_exact(mirror(), init=(1,), q=1)[0], det_dep(mirror(), ['w'], [0], 99, 1, [0, 1]),
         det_dep(mirror(), ['w'], [0], 0, 99, [0, 1]), det_hist(mirror(), ['not_signal'], [0], q=1),
         det_dep(mirror(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1], u='not_signal')), (DOM,) * 6)
    # 无限设定域成员判定的冻结身份
    def closure_member(v): return lambda a: v          # 闭包值不同，摘要随之不同
    da = report('Dep', mirror(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1], domain=('infinite', lambda x: True))
    db = report('Dep', mirror(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1], domain=('infinite', lambda x: False))
    dc = report('Dep', mirror(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1], domain=('infinite', lambda z: True))
    add('成员判定身份：不同实现摘要不同、Dom 不同；同一实现重复申报 Dom 相同；引用模块全局名而未申报标识 → ⊥_Dom，申报标识后记入 Dom；闭包值不同摘要不同',
        (da.Dom != db.Dom, da.Dom['C_op']['domain'] == report('Dep', mirror(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1], domain=('infinite', lambda x: True)).Dom['C_op']['domain'],
         da.Dom['C_op']['domain'] != dc.Dom['C_op']['domain'],
         det_dep(mirror(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1], domain=('infinite', _member_global)),
         report('Dep', mirror(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1], domain=('infinite', _member_global, '协议 G-1')).Dom['C_op']['domain'][1]['标识'],
         _member_id(('infinite', closure_member(True)))[1] != _member_id(('infinite', closure_member(False)))[1]),
        (True, True, True, DOM, '协议 G-1', True))
    # 引理二：界内副本在静置下由界内自身保持时判 1；静置即清零的副本不满足保持条件，判 0
    clear = Model(['m'], [1], [1], lambda st, u: {((1,) if u == 'w' else (0,)): 1.0}, (0,), ['w', 'rest'])
    add('引理二：镜像存储的界内副本在静置下保持，精确 Tr = 1；静置即清零的副本不满足保持条件，精确 Tr = 0（不与引理二相悖）',
        (det_tr_exact(mirror(), q=1, k=1)[0], det_tr_exact(clear, q=1, k=1)[0]), (1, 0))
    # 精确有理运算：TV 恰等于 r 时判 0（严格大于），r 略低于 TV 时判 1；Tr、Hist、Act、Dep 的概率路径与孪生协议都精确
    def coin(pr): return Model(['x'], [1], [1], lambda st, u: ({(0,): 1 - pr, (1,): pr} if u == 'w' and st == (0,) else {st: Fraction(1)}), (0,), ['w', 'rest'])
    def coin_s(pr): return Model(['x'], [1], [1], None, (0,), ['w', 'rest'], struct=(lambda st, u, w: ((1,) if (u == 'w' and w == 1) else st), {0: 1 - pr, 1: pr}))
    third = Fraction(1, 3); eps_ = Fraction(1, 10**12)
    add('精确阈值：TV = r = 1/3、1/7 时 Tr 判 0；r = 1/3 − 10⁻¹² 时判 1；结构表示（孪生协议）同；Hist 同',
        (det_tr_enum(coin(third), q=0, r=third, k=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_enum(coin(Fraction(1, 7)), q=0, r=Fraction(1, 7), k=1, H=1)[0],
         det_tr_enum(coin(third), q=0, r=third - eps_, k=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_enum(coin_s(third), q=0, r=third, k=1, H=1)[0],
         det_tr_enum(coin_s(third), q=0, r=third - eps_, k=1, H=1)[0], det_hist(coin(third), ['w'], [0], q=0, r=third),
         det_hist(coin(third), ['w'], [0], q=0, r=third - eps_)), (0, 0, 1, 0, 1, 0, 1))
    def coin_act(pr): return Model(['x', 'o'], [1, 0], [1, 1], lambda st, u: ({(0, 0): 1 - pr, (1, 0): pr} if (u == 'w' and st[0] == 0) else {(st[0], st[0] if u == 'p' else 0): Fraction(1)}),
                                   (0, 0), ['w', 'p', 'rest'], act=lambda st: st[1])
    add('精确阈值：Act_𝓟 (a) 款（探测后 act 的 TV = 1/3）在 r = 1/4 判 1、r = 1/3 判 0（此时 Tr 亦为 0）',
        (det_act(coin_act(third), q=0, r=Fraction(1, 4), k=1, H=1, P=1, probes=[('p',)]), det_act(coin_act(third), q=0, r=third, k=1, H=1, P=1, probes=[('p',)])), (1, 0))
    # 撤读的阶段化运行：历史 → 静置 → 撤供 → 探测；静读 Act_h = 1、撤读 Act_h = 0
    def wd(s_, u):
        y, o, g = s_
        return {(1 if u == 'w' else y, y * g if u == 'p' else 0, g): Fraction(1)}
    Aw = Model(['y', 'o', 'g'], [1, 1, 1], [1, 1, 1], wd, (0, 0, 1), ['rest', 'w', 'p'], act=lambda s_: s_[1], cut=lambda s_: {(s_[0], 0, 0): Fraction(1)})
    seen_w = set(); Aw_r = recording(Aw, seen_w)
    add('撤读的阶段化运行：Hist^静 = Hist^撤 = 1；Act_h^静 = 1，Act_h^撤 = 0（探测施于撤供后的状态）',
        (det_hist(Aw, ['w'], {0}, q=1, reading='静'), det_hist(Aw, ['w'], {0}, q=1, reading='撤'),
         det_act_h(Aw, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, reading='静', probes=[('p',)]), det_act_h(Aw, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, reading='撤', probes=[('p',)])), (1, 1, 1, 0))
    add('阶段化运行器：序列中间的撤供步按撤供律执行（撤供后 g = 0，再施 p 时 o = 0）',
        run_pair(Aw, ['w', 'rest', CUT, 'p'], ['rest', 'rest', CUT, 'p']), ({(1, 0, 0): 1}, {(0, 0, 0): 1}))
    # 监控自动机的扩展界（定理一 (c)）
    MON20 = (0, {(i, u): i + 1 for i in range(20) for u in ('w', 'rest')}, {20})
    m20 = det_tr_exact(mirror(), q=0, k=1, mon=MON20)
    add('监控扩展界：只接受恰长 20 的 𝓗（21 状态），最短见证史长 20 ≤ 搜索所得界 21²·B；已搜扩展节点数为事后计数；无监控时界不乘因子',
        (m20[0], m20[1], m20[1] <= m20[4], m20[4] == 21 ** 2 * (m20[2] + m20[2] * m20[3] * 1), det_tr_exact(mirror(), q=0, k=1)[4] == 6, m20[5] > 0), (1, 20, True, True, True, True))
    add('监控扩展界：空接受集时 Tr = 0；长合法前缀（要求先有 5 个 rest）时最短见证史长 6',
        (det_tr_exact(mirror(), q=0, k=1, mon=(0, {(0, 'w'): 0, (0, 'rest'): 0}, set()))[0],
         det_tr_exact(mirror(), q=0, k=1, mon=(0, {**{(i, 'rest'): i + 1 for i in range(5)}, (5, 'w'): 5, (5, 'rest'): 5}, {5}))[1]), (0, 6))
    # 引理七的自足见证：冗余形成。a、b 都把 x 写为 1；实际史 (a, b) 与参照史 (rest, b) 末态相同，Hist = 0；而实际 a 当拍留下 0 → 1 的有效改变
    red = Model(['x'], [1], [1], lambda st, u: {((1,) if u in ('a', 'b') else st): Fraction(1)}, (0,), ['a', 'b', 'rest'])
    add('引理七（冗余形成）：Hist((a, b) 对 (rest, b)) = 0，而实际史第一拍 a 使 x 由 0 变 1（与参照史第一拍后的状态不同）',
        (det_hist(red, ['a', 'b'], [0], q=1), run_pair(red, ['a'], ['rest'])), (0, ({(1,): 1}, {(0,): 1})))
    # 撤供哨兵与输入名分离：申报的普通输入 '__cut' 只交给转移律；真正撤供走撤供律
    sig = lambda name: Model(['x'], [1], [1], lambda st, u: {((1,) if u == name else st): Fraction(1)}, (0,), ['rest', name], cut=lambda st: {(0,): Fraction(1)})
    sig_s = lambda name: Model(['x'], [1], [1], None, (0,), ['rest', name], struct=(lambda st, u, w: ((1,) if u == name else st), {0: 1}, lambda st, w: (0,)))
    add('输入名 __cut 与撤供哨兵不冲突：转移核与结构表示两路径，__cut 信号与改名 write 都判 Tr^静 = 1；真正撤供（清零）下 Tr^撤 = 0',
        tuple((det_tr_exact(f(nm), q=1, k=1)[0], det_tr_enum(f(nm), q=1, k=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_enum(f(nm), q=1, k=1, H=1, reading='撤')[0])
              for f in (sig, sig_s) for nm in ('__cut', 'write')), ((1, 1, 0),) * 4)
    # 撤读 (b) 款的写入核对用全部界内分量
    def ss(s_, u):
        y, o, z = s_
        if u == 'w': return {(1, 1, z): Fraction(1)}
        if u == 'p': return {(y, 2 * y, 1): Fraction(1)}
        return {s_: Fraction(1)}
    Mz = Model(['y', 'o', 'z'], [1, 0, 0], [1, 1, 1], ss, (0, 0, 0), ['rest', 'w', 'p'], act=lambda s_: s_[1], cut=lambda s_: {s_: Fraction(1)})
    add('撤读 (b) 款核对全部界内分量：p 在申报块外持久写入 z 时 Act_h^撤 = 0；自发流道（p 不写入任何界内位、增量差 2）Act_h^撤 = 1',
        (det_act_h(Mz, ['w'], [0], q=1, reading='撤', P=1, probes=[('p',)], r=Fraction(1, 20), r_act=Fraction(1, 20)),
         det_act_h(spontaneous_channel(), ['w'], [0], q=1, reading='撤', P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('p',)])), (0, 1))
    # 成员判定摘要含仅关键字缺省值
    def kw_factory(flag):
        def member(a, *, allowed=flag): return allowed
        return member
    kT = report('Dep', dep_unsampled(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1, 2], domain=('infinite', kw_factory(True)))
    kF = report('Dep', dep_unsampled(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1, 2], domain=('infinite', kw_factory(False)))
    kT2 = report('Dep', dep_unsampled(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1, 2], domain=('infinite', kw_factory(True)))
    add('成员判定摘要含仅关键字缺省值：allowed=True 与 False 的 Dom 不同（判词 界内 0 与 ⊥_Dom）；同一冻结缺省值重复申报 Dom 相同',
        (kT.Dom != kF.Dom, kT.v, kF.Code, kT.Dom == kT2.Dom), (True, 0, DOM, True))
    # 元组与列表的历史同义
    rl, rt = report('Hist', mirror(), ['w'], [0], q=1), report('Hist', mirror(), ('w',), [0], q=1)
    add('元组与列表的实际史同义：Hist、Act_h、Dep 判值相同，Hist 报告的 Dom 相同，无未处理异常',
        (rl.v == rt.v == 1, rl.Dom == rt.Dom,
         det_act_h(mirror(), ('w',), [0], q=1, P=1, probes=[('rest',)]) == det_act_h(mirror(), ['w'], [0], q=1, P=1, probes=[('rest',)]),
         det_dep(dep_unsampled(), ('w',), [0], 0, 1, [0, 1, 2, 3]) == det_dep(dep_unsampled(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1, 2, 3]) == 1), (True, True, True, True))
    # 搜索所得界与先验界分列
    rr_ = report('Tr_exact', mirror(), q=0, k=1, mon=MON20)
    add('精确判定的 Read：搜索所得史长上界 2646、已搜扩展节点数 100 分列；先验史长界未提供（须申报 N 与 |S_in| 的上界）',
        (rr_.Read['搜索所得史长上界'], rr_.Read['已搜扩展节点数'], rr_.Read['先验史长界'].startswith('未提供')), (2646, 100, True))
    # Dep 的数值读数精确：浮点读数在边界转为有理数后再相减、求均值、平移与比较
    def dstep(st, u, one=1.0, tenth=.1):
        x, y, z = st
        return {(0, y, 1): 1} if u == 'w' else {((one if z else tenth) * y, y, z): 1}
    Mf = Model(['x', 'y', 'z'], [1, 1, 1], [1, 1, 1], dstep, (0, 0, 0), ['w', 'rest'])
    Mq = Model(['x', 'y', 'z'], [1, 1, 1], [1, 1, 1], lambda st, u: dstep(st, u, Fraction(1), Fraction(1, 10)), (0, 0, 0), ['w', 'rest'])
    tiny = Fraction(1, 10**12)
    add('Dep 均值差恰为 9/10：浮点读数模型在 r_μ = 9/10 判 0、9/10 − 10⁻¹² 判 1、9/10 + 10⁻¹² 判 0；与全 Fraction 模型逐一相同',
        tuple((det_dep(m_, ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1], r_mu=rr, r_rho=1)) for m_ in (Mf, Mq) for rr in (Fraction(9, 10), Fraction(9, 10) - tiny, Fraction(9, 10) + tiny)),
        (0, 1, 0, 0, 1, 0))
    Mpre = Model(['x', 'y'], [1, 1], [1, 1], lambda st, u: {((.3 if st[1] == 1 else .2), st[1]): 1}, (.2, 0), ['rest'])
    Mmix = Model(['x', 'y'], [1, 1], [1, 1], lambda st, u: ({(.3, 1): .5, (.2, 1): .5} if st[1] == 1 else {(.2, 0): 1.0}), (.2, 0), ['rest'])
    ep, em = y_effect(Mpre, (.2, 0), 0, 1, 0, 1), y_effect(Mmix, (.2, 0), 0, 1, 0, 1)
    add('Dep 读数的非零前态与多点分布：前态 0.2、后态 0.3 时 Δμ 精确为 1/10（浮点相减为 0.09999…）；多点分布 Δμ = 1/20、ρ = 1；全部为有理数',
        (ep[0], em[0], em[1], all(isinstance(v, (int, Fraction)) for v in ep + em)), (Fraction(1, 10), Fraction(1, 20), 1, True))
    # act 的浮点读数同样精确：(b) 款增量差恰为 r_act 时判 0
    def chan(table):
        def step(st, u):
            y, o = st
            if u == 'w': y = clip(y + 1, 0, 2)
            return {(y, 2 * y + 1 if u == 'p' else y): 1}
        return Model(['depth', 'flow'], [1, 0], [1, 1], step, (0, 0), ['w', 'p', 'rest'], act=lambda st: table[st[1]])
    fl, fr = {0: 0, 1: .1, 2: .2, 3: .3, 5: .5}, {k: Fraction(v).limit_denominator(10) for k, v in {0: 0, 1: .1, 2: .2, 3: .3, 5: .5}.items()}
    add('act 浮点读数（读数为十进制字面值）：增量差恰为 r_act = 1/10 时 (b) 款判 0，r_act 略低判 1；0.1 与 Fraction(1, 10) 两种读数模型相同',
        tuple(det_act_h(chan(tb), ['w'], [0], q=1, P=1, r_act=ra, probes=[('p',)]) for tb in (fl, fr) for ra in (Fraction(1, 10), Fraction(1, 10) - tiny)),
        (0, 1, 0, 1))
    add('读数政策：模型自身的浮点运算按其产生的值解释——0.1·3 产生 0.30000000000000004，取其十进制表示的精确值，不改写为 3/10',
        (_qn(0.1 * 3) == Fraction('0.30000000000000004'), _qn(0.3) == Fraction(3, 10)), (True, True))
    # 有效模型表示：起点在构造时规范化一次，各运行入口同一起点；外生扰动保持申报键，各路径同一份已校验分布
    def a_model(cmp_val, init):
        return Model(['a', 'x'], [1, 1], [1, 1], lambda st, u: {(st[0], 1 if (u == 'w' and st[0] == cmp_val) else st[1]): 1}, init, ['rest', 'w'])
    rA = [(det_tr_enum(a_model(.1, ini), q=1, k=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_exact(a_model(.1, ini), q=1, k=1)[:2]) for ini in ((.1, 0), (Fraction(1, 10), 0))]
    rC = [(det_tr_enum(a_model(Fraction(1, 10), ini), q=1, k=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_exact(a_model(Fraction(1, 10), ini), q=1, k=1)[:2]) for ini in ((.1, 0), (Fraction(1, 10), 0))]
    expl = det_tr_exact(a_model(Fraction(1, 10), (0, 0)), q=1, k=1, init=(.1, 0))[:2]
    add('同一起点表示：起点 0.1 与 Fraction(1, 10) 同为有理 1/10；转移按有理状态求值（与浮点 0.1 比较为假：枚举、精确都判 0；与 1/10 比较：都判 1、最短史长 1）；显式起点与缺省起点一致',
        (rA, rC, expl), ([(0, (0, None))] * 2, [(1, (1, 1))] * 2, (1, 1)))
    Sd = Model(['x'], [1], [1], None, (0,), ['rest', 'w'], struct=(lambda st, u, om: ((1,) if (u == 'w' and om == .1) else st), {.1: 1}))
    add('外生扰动保持申报键：φ 直接调用、净转移（精确判定）与孪生协议（枚举）都以申报值 0.1 调用 φ，判 1',
        (Sd.struct[0]((0,), 'w', .1), Sd.step((0,), 'w'), det_tr_exact(Sd, q=1, k=1)[:2], det_tr_enum(Sd, q=1, k=1, H=1)[0]), ((1,), {(1,): 1}, (1, 1), 1))
    Scut = Model(['x'], [1], [1], None, (0,), ['rest', 'w'], struct=(lambda st, u, om: ((1,) if u == 'w' else st), {.1: 1}, lambda st, om: ((0,) if om == .1 else st)))
    add('撤供结构同用申报扰动键：cut_φ 以 0.1 调用，撤供清零，Tr^撤 = 0、Tr^静 = 1（枚举与精确一致）',
        (det_tr_enum(Scut, q=1, k=1, H=1, reading='撤')[0], det_tr_exact(Scut, q=1, k=1, reading='撤')[0], det_tr_enum(Scut, q=1, k=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_exact(Scut, q=1, k=1)[0]), (0, 0, 1, 1))
    # 原始概率先逐项校验，再合并或推送
    Bk = Model(['x'], [1], [1], lambda st, u: ({(.1,): Fraction(-1, 2), (Fraction(1, 10),): Fraction(3, 2)} if u == 'w' else {st: 1}), (0,), ['rest', 'w'])
    Ek = Model(['x'], [1], [1], None, (0,), ['rest', 'w'], struct=(lambda st, u, om: st, {0: Fraction(-1, 2), 1: Fraction(3, 2)}))
    Pk = Model(['x'], [1], [1], lambda st, u: ({(.1,): Fraction(1, 2), (Fraction(1, 10),): Fraction(1, 2)} if u == 'w' else {st: 1}), (0,), ['rest', 'w'])
    add('原始负概率不因合并或推送而抵消：状态键合并（枚举、精确）与外生扰动推送（枚举、精确）都判 ⊥_Dom；正概率 0.1 与 1/10 两后继合并为概率 1，照常判 1',
        (det_tr_enum(Bk, q=1, k=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_exact(Bk, q=1, k=1)[0], det_tr_enum(Ek, q=1, k=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_exact(Ek, q=1, k=1)[0],
         Pk.step((0,), 'w'), det_tr_exact(Pk, q=1, k=1)[0]), (DOM, DOM, DOM, DOM, {(Fraction(1, 10),): 1}, 1))
    # 交叉核验：确定模型上枚举与精确判定在覆盖史长时判值与最短见证一致
    def cross(M_, q_, k_):
        g = det_tr_exact(M_, q=q_, k=k_)
        H_ = g[1] if g[0] == 1 else 4
        e = det_tr_enum(M_, q=q_, k=k_, H=H_)
        return g[0] == e[0] and (g[0] != 1 or len(e[1][0]) == g[1])
    det_models = [(echo(), 6, 1), (conservative_pair(), 6, 1), (mirror(), 6, 1), (mutual_external(), 4, 1), (gate_once(), 1, 1), (long_prep(3), 1, 1),
                  (hybrid(3), 2, 1), (table_model(), 1, 2), (refresh_dram(), 2, 1), (spontaneous_channel(), 1, 1), (a_model(Fraction(1, 10), (.1, 0)), 1, 1), (Sd, 1, 1)]
    add(f'交叉核验：{len(det_models)} 个确定模型（含浮点起点与结构表示）上，枚举与精确判定的判值一致，判 1 时最短见证史长一致',
        all(cross(*m_) for m_ in det_models), True)
    add('Dep 设定水平与有限 V_y 按同一有效表示比较：申报 V_y = {1/10, 1}、实测水平 {0.1, 1} 不判 ⊥_Dom，与全 Fraction 写法同值',
        det_dep(dep_unsampled(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [.1, 1], domain=[Fraction(1, 10), 1]) == det_dep(dep_unsampled(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [Fraction(1, 10), 1], domain=[Fraction(1, 10), 1]) != DOM, True)
    # 构造时已查出的不合法外生扰动分布进入统一入口：有无不同合法史对，一切公开判定都在求值前判 ⊥_Dom
    MON_REST = (0, {(0, 'rest'): 0}, {0})
    bad_om = {'负概率': {0: Fraction(-1, 2), 1: Fraction(3, 2)}, '和不为 1': {0: Fraction(4, 5)}, '空分布': {}, '非有限': {0: float('inf')}}
    def om_model(om): return Model(['x'], [1], [1], None, (0,), ['rest', 'w'], struct=(lambda st, u, w: st, om))
    res_om = []
    for om in bad_om.values():
        Mo = om_model(om)
        for mon_ in (MON_REST, None):
            res_om.append((validate_layer1_decl(Mo), det_tr_enum(Mo, q=1, k=1, H=2, mon=mon_)[0], det_tr_exact(Mo, q=1, k=1, mon=mon_)[0],
                           det_act(Mo, q=1, k=1, H=2, mon=mon_, probes=[]), det_hist(Mo, ['rest'], [0], q=1, mon=mon_), report('Tr', Mo, q=1, k=1, H=2, mon=mon_).v))
    add('不合法外生扰动分布（负概率、和不为 1、空分布、非有限）：受限合法史（rest*，无不同史对）与全序列两种情形下，入口、Tr 枚举与精确、Act、Hist、报告都判 ⊥_Dom；合法分布在 rest* 下照常判 0',
        (all(r_ == (DOM, DOM, DOM, DOM, DOM, '⊥') for r_ in res_om), det_tr_enum(om_model({0: 1}), q=1, k=1, H=2, mon=MON_REST)[0], det_tr_exact(om_model({0: 1}), q=1, k=1, mon=MON_REST)[0]),
        (True, 0, 0))
    add('线性加性 Dep', det_dep(linear_additive(), ['w', 'w'], {0, 1}, 0, 1, [0, 1, 2]), 0)
    add('随机末态 Dep → ⊥_Obs', det_dep(prob_write(), ['w'], {0}, 0, 0, [0, 1]), OBS)
    # 撤供下的历史留存
    M = refresh_dram()
    add('刷新维持电荷 Hist^撤/Hist^静', (det_hist(M, ['w'], {0}, q=2, reading='撤', init=(0,)),
                                     det_hist(M, ['w'], {0}, q=2, reading='静', init=(0,))), (0, 1))
    return T
if __name__ == '__main__':
    ok = n = 0
    for name, got, exp, mark in tests():
        print(f'{mark} {name:58s} 判={got} 期望={exp}'); ok += mark == '✓'; n += 1
    print(f'合计 {ok}/{n} 项符合')
~~~

A.2　layer1\_second.py　第一层 Tr 判定的第二实现：暴力枚举与成对自动机（定理一、二，引理二）

~~~python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""沉积场判定式 附录 A.2：第一层 Tr 判定的第二实现（暴力枚举与成对自动机两法互证；与附录 A.1 分别编写）。
见证模型：M1（定理一 (a2) 的 N²(k+1) 失效）、M2（定理一 (b)）、M3（定理二 (b)）、M4（引理二）。
模型: T(x,u) -> x'；状态为元组；inside = 界内分量下标，outside = 界外分量下标。
钳定: 每步各自转移后，把 h' 侧状态的界外分量改为 h 侧同一步的界外分量。
见证: h != h'，差异位置数 <= k，史长 <= H，q 步（钳定）静置后读数不同。
读数 read(x) 默认取界内分量（读法「静」）。
"""
import itertools
from collections import deque

class Model:
    def __init__(self, name, s0, alphabet, T, inside, outside, read=None):
        self.name, self.s0, self.alphabet, self.T = name, s0, alphabet, T
        self.inside, self.outside = inside, outside
        self.read = read or (lambda x: tuple(x[i] for i in inside))
    def clamp(self, sp, s):
        if not self.outside:
            return sp
        y = list(sp)
        for i in self.outside:
            y[i] = s[i]
        return tuple(y)
    def step(self, s, sp, u, up, clamped):
        s1 = self.T(s, u)
        sp1 = self.T(sp, up)
        if clamped:
            sp1 = self.clamp(sp1, s1)
        return s1, sp1
    def rest_differs(self, s, sp, q, clamped):
        for _ in range(q):
            s, sp = self.step(s, sp, 'rest', 'rest', clamped)
        return self.read(s) != self.read(sp)
    def reachable(self):
        seen = {self.s0}
        dq = deque([self.s0])
        while dq:
            x = dq.popleft()
            for u in self.alphabet:
                y = self.T(x, u)
                if y not in seen:
                    seen.add(y)
                    dq.append(y)
        return seen

# ---------------- 方法一：暴力枚举 ----------------
def run_pair(M, h, hp, q, clamped):
    s = sp = M.s0
    for u, up in zip(h, hp):
        s, sp = M.step(s, sp, u, up, clamped)
    return M.rest_differs(s, sp, q, clamped)

def brute_explicit(M, q, k, H, clamped=True):
    """逐对显式枚举（无任何前缀共享），返回最短见证 (长度, h, h') 或 None。"""
    A = M.alphabet
    for L in range(1, H + 1):
        for h in itertools.product(A, repeat=L):
            for d in range(1, k + 1):
                for pos in itertools.combinations(range(L), d):
                    choices = [[a for a in A if a != h[p]] for p in pos]
                    for sub in itertools.product(*choices):
                        hp = list(h)
                        for p, a in zip(pos, sub):
                            hp[p] = a
                        if run_pair(M, h, tuple(hp), q, clamped):
                            return L, h, tuple(hp)
    return None

def brute_tree(M, q, k, H, clamped=True):
    """穷举一切史对（DFS 共享前缀计算，但不合并状态，仍是逐史对枚举）。
    返回每个史长 L 是否存在见证的最短 L 与一个见证。"""
    A = M.alphabet
    best = [H + 1, None]
    count = [0]
    path = []
    def dfs(s, sp, j, d):
        for u in A:
            for up in A:
                jj = j + (u != up)
                if jj > k:
                    continue
                s1, sp1 = M.step(s, sp, u, up, clamped)
                count[0] += 1
                path.append((u, up))
                if jj >= 1 and d + 1 < best[0] and M.rest_differs(s1, sp1, q, clamped):
                    best[0], best[1] = d + 1, list(path)
                if d + 1 < min(H, best[0] - 1):
                    dfs(s1, sp1, jj, d + 1)
                path.pop()
    dfs(M.s0, M.s0, 0, 0)
    if best[1] is None:
        return None, None, None, count[0]
    h = tuple(a for a, _ in best[1])
    hp = tuple(b for _, b in best[1])
    return best[0], h, hp, count[0]

# ---------------- 方法二：成对自动机 BFS ----------------
def bfs(M, q, k, clamped=True):
    start = (M.s0, M.s0, 0)
    parent = {start: None}
    depth = {start: 0}
    dq = deque([start])
    accept = None
    A = M.alphabet
    while dq:
        node = dq.popleft()
        s, sp, j = node
        if j >= 1 and accept is None and M.rest_differs(s, sp, q, clamped):
            accept = node
            # 不提前终止：继续搜完整图以统计节点数与 |I'|
        for u in A:
            for up in A:
                jj = j + (u != up)
                if jj > k:
                    continue
                s1, sp1 = M.step(s, sp, u, up, clamped)
                nn = (s1, sp1, jj)
                if nn not in parent:
                    parent[nn] = (node, u, up)
                    depth[nn] = depth[node] + 1
                    dq.append(nn)
    # BFS 出队序即深度序，第一个被检出的接受节点深度最小
    Iprime = {tuple(sp[i] for i in M.inside) for (s, sp, j) in parent if j >= 1}
    res = dict(nodes=len(parent), Iprime=len(Iprime), Tr=0, L=None, h=None, hp=None)
    if accept is not None:
        h, hp = [], []
        n = accept
        while parent[n] is not None:
            p, u, up = parent[n]
            h.append(u); hp.append(up); n = p
        res.update(Tr=1, L=depth[accept], h=tuple(reversed(h)), hp=tuple(reversed(hp)))
    return res

# ---------------- 模型 ----------------
def T_M1(x, u):
    m, E = x
    if u == 'w':
        return (1, 1)
    if m != E:
        if u == 'a':
            return (min(12, m + 1), E)
        if u == 'rest' and m < 12:
            return (E, E)
        return (m, E)
    return (m, E)

M1 = Model('M1 钳定混合态', (0, 0), ['w', 'a', 'rest'], T_M1, [0], [1])

def make_M2(n=6, read_x_only=False):
    def T(x, u):
        c, xx = x
        if u == 'a':
            return (min(n, c + 1), xx)
        if u == 'w' and c >= n:
            return (c, 1)
        return (0, xx)
    read = (lambda s: (s[1],)) if read_x_only else None
    return Model('M2 长预备史 n=%d%s' % (n, ' 只比x' if read_x_only else ' 静'),
                 (0, 0), ['a', 'w', 'rest'], T, [0, 1], [], read)

M3_TAB = {
    (0, 0): {'a': (0, 0), 'b': (1, 0), 'rest': (0, 0)},
    (0, 1): {'a': (0, 1), 'b': (0, 0), 'rest': (0, 0)},
    (1, 0): {'a': (0, 1), 'b': (0, 0), 'rest': (0, 1)},
    (1, 1): {'a': (1, 0), 'b': (0, 1), 'rest': (1, 1)},
}
M3 = Model('M3 TAB 反例', (0, 0), ['a', 'b', 'rest'], lambda x, u: M3_TAB[x][u], [0], [1])

def T_M4(x, u):
    m, E = x
    m2, E2 = E, m
    if u == 'w':
        m2 = 1
    return (m2, E2)

M4 = Model('M4 互相回写外存', (0, 0), ['w', 'rest'], T_M4, [0], [1])

# ---------------- 主程序 ----------------
def fmt(r):
    s = 'Tr=%d 节点数=%d |I\'|=%d' % (r['Tr'], r['nodes'], r['Iprime'])
    if r['Tr']:
        s += ' 最短见证史长=%d h=%s h\'=%s' % (r['L'], ','.join(r['h']), ','.join(r['hp']))
    return s

def trace(M, h, hp, q, clamped=True):
    s = sp = M.s0
    lines = ['  初 h:%s h\':%s' % (s, sp)]
    for u, up in list(zip(h, hp)) + [('rest', 'rest')] * q:
        s1 = M.T(s, u); raw = M.T(sp, up)
        sp1 = M.clamp(raw, s1) if clamped else raw
        lines.append('  %s/%s -> h:%s  h\':%s(未钳定)->%s' % (u, up, s1, raw, sp1))
        s, sp = s1, sp1
    lines.append('  读数 h=%s h\'=%s 不同=%s' % (M.read(s), M.read(sp), M.read(s) != M.read(sp)))
    return '\n'.join(lines)

def main():
    out = []
    P = out.append
    # ---- M1 ----
    P('=== M1 钳定混合态, q=2, k=1 ===')
    N = len(M1.reachable())
    P('可达状态 N=%d  %s' % (N, sorted(M1.reachable())))
    r = bfs(M1, 2, 1, True)
    P('BFS 钳定: ' + fmt(r))
    P('N^2(k+1)=%d  节点数 %s 该值' % (N * N * 2, '超过' if r['nodes'] > N * N * 2 else '未超过'))
    P('N + N*|I\'|*k = %d  (>= 最短见证 %s ? %s)' % (N + N * r['Iprime'] * 1, r['L'],
                                                  N + N * r['Iprime'] >= (r['L'] or 0)))
    P(trace(M1, r['h'], r['hp'], 2))
    P('BFS 不钳定(参考): ' + fmt(bfs(M1, 2, 1, False)))
    for H in (11, 12, 13):
        L, h, hp, cnt = brute_tree(M1, 2, 1, H, True)
        P('暴力(树枚举) H=%d: 最短见证=%s  %s  (枚举史对前缀 %d)' % (
            H, L, ('h=%s h\'=%s' % (','.join(h), ','.join(hp))) if L else '', cnt))
    P('暴力(逐对显式) H=9: %s' % (brute_explicit(M1, 2, 1, 9, True),))
    # ---- M2 ----
    for xo in (False, True):
        M2 = make_M2(6, xo)
        P('\n=== %s, q=1, k=1 ===' % M2.name)
        P('可达状态 N=%d' % len(M2.reachable()))
        r = bfs(M2, 1, 1, True)
        P('BFS: ' + fmt(r))
        P(trace(M2, r['h'], r['hp'], 1))
        P('暴力(逐对显式) H=6: %s' % (brute_explicit(M2, 1, 1, 6, True),))
        P('暴力(逐对显式) H=7: %s' % (brute_explicit(M2, 1, 1, 7, True),))
    # ---- M3 ----
    P('\n=== M3 TAB 反例, q=1 ===')
    P('可达状态 N=%d  %s' % (len(M3.reachable()), sorted(M3.reachable())))
    for clamped in (True, False):
        for k in (1, 2):
            r = bfs(M3, 1, k, clamped)
            P('BFS %s k=%d: %s' % ('钳定' if clamped else '不钳定', k, fmt(r)))
            L, h, hp, cnt = brute_tree(M3, 1, k, 8, clamped)
            P('   暴力(树枚举) H=8: 最短见证=%s %s' % (
                L, ('h=%s h\'=%s' % (','.join(h), ','.join(hp))) if L else ''))
            P('   暴力(逐对显式) H=6: %s' % (brute_explicit(M3, 1, k, 6, clamped),))
    for h, hp in ((('b', 'a'), ('a', 'b')), (('a', 'b'), ('b', 'a'))):
        P('史对 h=%s h\'=%s 钳定 q=1 是否见证: %s' % (h, hp, run_pair(M3, h, hp, 1, True)))
        P(trace(M3, h, hp, 1))
        P('  同史对不钳定: %s' % run_pair(M3, h, hp, 1, False))
    # ---- M4 ----
    P('\n=== M4 互相回写外存, q=4, k=1 ===')
    P('可达状态 N=%d  %s' % (len(M4.reachable()), sorted(M4.reachable())))
    for clamped in (False, True):
        r = bfs(M4, 4, 1, clamped)
        P('BFS %s: %s' % ('钳定' if clamped else '不钳定', fmt(r)))
        if r['Tr']:
            P(trace(M4, r['h'], r['hp'], 4, clamped))
        P('   暴力(逐对显式) H=10: %s' % (brute_explicit(M4, 4, 1, 10, clamped),))
    print('\n'.join(out))

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.3　t3cd.py　定理二 (c)(d) 的精确分布计算

~~~python
"""沉积场判定式 附录 A.3：定理二 (c)(d) 的精确分布计算（不依赖采样）。"""
import itertools
from collections import defaultdict
def tv(a,b): ks=set(a)|set(b); return .5*sum(abs(a.get(k,0)-b.get(k,0)) for k in ks)
def run(step, s0, h):
    d={s0:1.0}
    for u in h:
        n=defaultdict(float)
        for s,p in d.items():
            for t,q in step(s,u).items(): n[t]+=p*q
        d=dict(n)
    return d
# (c) 随机写入：x∈{0,1}，w 使 0→1 概率 ε
eps=0.05
st=lambda s,u: ({(1,):eps,(0,):1-eps} if (u=='w' and s[0]==0) else {s:1.0})
for j in (1,2,3):
    d1=run(st,(0,),['w']*j+['rest']); d2=run(st,(0,),['rest']*j+['rest'])
    print(f'(c) j={j} 位置差异 TV={tv(d1,d2):.4f}  解析 1-(1-ε)^j={1-(1-eps)**j:.4f}')
# 全搜索：k 个位置不同、史长 ≤ 4 的最大 TV
def maxTV(step,s0,alpha,k,H,q=1):
    best=0
    for L in range(1,H+1):
        for h in itertools.product(alpha,repeat=L):
            for kk in range(1,min(k,L)+1):
                for pos in itertools.combinations(range(L),kk):
                    for alt in itertools.product(alpha,repeat=kk):
                        h2=list(h)
                        for p,a in zip(pos,alt): h2[p]=a
                        if tuple(h2)==h: continue
                        best=max(best,tv(run(step,s0,list(h)+['rest']*q),run(step,s0,h2+['rest']*q)))
    return best
print('(c) r=0.08：k=1 最大 TV（H≤4）=%.4f；k=2 最大 TV=%.4f' % (maxTV(st,(0,),['w','rest'],1,4), maxTV(st,(0,),['w','rest'],2,4)))
# (d) 门控时钟：c∈{0..3} 每步 +1 mod 4；c=0 时 w 以 0.04 使 x+1（x≤3）
G,e=4,0.04
def gst(s,u):
    c,x=s; c2=(c+1)%G
    if u=='w' and c==0: return {(c2,min(3,x+1)):e,(c2,x):1-e}
    return {(c2,x):1.0}
r=0.05
def contig_max(Lseg,H):
    best=0
    for L in range(Lseg,H+1):
        for start in range(L-Lseg+1):
            for seg in itertools.product(['w','rest'],repeat=Lseg):
                h=['rest']*L; h2=list(h)
                for i,a in enumerate(seg): h2[start+i]=a
                if h2==h: continue
                best=max(best,tv(run(gst,(0,0),h+['rest']),run(gst,(0,0),h2+['rest'])))
    return best
print('(d) 位置读：k=2 最大 TV（H≤9）=%.4f' % maxTV(gst,(0,0),['w','rest'],2,9))
for Lseg in (1,2,3,4,5):
    print(f'(d) 连段读：长度 {Lseg} 的连段（基线全静置，H≤9）最大 TV = {contig_max(Lseg,9):.4f}')
~~~

A.4　mtm.py　机制转移模型、机制级申报入口（与上游 F 定义域一致）、三级观测、扰动覆盖、申报与负判前件清单、机制级辨识、⊥ 原因码、五元组报告适配器（原子报告同源）与界内自反馈型判词（3.5，4.6，4.8，定理六，引理 6.1）

~~~python
"""沉积场判定式 附录 A.4：机制转移模型 MTM、三级观测与机制级辨识程序。
MTM 是带输入的机制模型：每拍的输入 u 决定界外脉冲，并以门控因子缩放各机制的数值律；
固定一条输入史后，每拍每个机制是《场具化数理式》F 层的一条可分机制，故实际史的三项职责
直接交由《场具化数理式》v1.0 附录 A.1 的求值器 field_final.run 计算（地面真值）。
三类观测：
  Y1  剖面级：净转移 N(t, s, u)（任意状态、任意输入）与活动读出 act(s)；
  Y2  机制级：逐机制敲除 KO、迹应位局部设定 LOC、读出 Out；
  Ydecl 申报：逐拍机制清单（名、载体、对象、是否运行）与清单完备性 INV-c。
"""
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import os as _os
sys.path.insert(0, _os.path.dirname(_os.path.abspath(__file__)))   # field_final.py 与本文件同目录（与《场具化数理式》v1.0 附录 A.1 逐字相同）
from field_final import Spec, Pulse, Process, Link, run, exact
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Mech:
    name: str
    target: str
    inside: bool                 # 载体在 B 内
    law: str                     # constant / linear / bilinear
    reads: tuple = ()
    k: Fr = Fr(0)
    b: Fr = Fr(0)
    gate: tuple = ()             # ((u, factor), ...)；未列出的输入因子为 1；因子 0 表示该拍不运行
    beats: tuple | None = None   # None 表示每拍都可运行
    def factor(self, u):
        for uu, f in self.gate:
            if uu == u:
                return Fr(f)
        return Fr(1)
    def runs(self, t, u):
        return (self.beats is None or t in self.beats) and self.factor(u) != 0
    def value(self, s, u):
        g = self.factor(u); k = self.k * g; b = self.b * g
        if self.law == 'constant':
            return b
        if self.law == 'linear':
            return b + k * sum(s[r] for r in self.reads)
        return b + k * s[self.reads[0]] * s[self.reads[1]]

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MTM:
    sites: tuple
    s0: tuple
    T: tuple
    eps: Fr
    t0: int
    t1: int
    pulses: tuple = ()           # ((u, ((target, amount), ...)), ...)，均为界外信号脉冲
    mechs: tuple = ()
    def pulses_of(self, u):
        for uu, ps in self.pulses:
            if uu == u:
                return ps
        return ()
    def beats(self):
        return list(range(self.t0 - 1, self.t1 + 1))

# ---------- 地面真值：实际史 → 数理式 F 规格 → field_final ----------
def to_spec(M, hist, T=None):
    """参考真值的 F 规格：按已知模型结构生成——读取位即 Mech.reads，读取认证置 1，证据边按读取位逐条置 1。
    它是「已知模型的 F 编码」，供核对 Y₂ 反演是否复现该编码；不是由独立物质观测取得的读取与途径认证。"""
    assert len(hist) == len(M.beats())
    pulses = []; procs = []; links = []
    for t, u in zip(M.beats(), hist):
        for j, (tg, a) in enumerate(M.pulses_of(u)):
            pulses.append(Pulse(f'{u}@{t}#{j}', t, tg, a, True, True))
        for m in M.mechs:
            if not m.runs(t, u):
                continue
            g = m.factor(u)
            nm = f'{m.name}@{t}'
            procs.append(Process(nm, t, m.target, m.inside, m.law, tuple(m.reads), m.k * g, m.b * g, True, True))
            if t >= M.t0:
                for r in m.reads:
                    links.append(Link(f'state:{r}:{t}', nm, True))
    return Spec(tuple(M.sites), tuple(M.s0), tuple(range(M.t0, M.t1 + 1)), tuple(pulses), tuple(procs),
                tuple(links), True, True, True, M.eps, tuple(T if T is not None else M.T))

def truth(M, hist, T=None):
    r = run(to_spec(M, hist, T))
    return {k: r[k] for k in ('trace', 'part', 'hdyn', 'Q')}

# ---------- Y1：剖面级观测 ----------
def net(M, t, s, u, drop=None):
    """净转移；drop 为敲除的机制名或脉冲名 'pulse#j'（仅供 Y2 使用）。数理式 4.2：只加有效分项。"""
    eps = M.eps; new = dict(s)
    for j, (tg, a) in enumerate(M.pulses_of(u)):
        a = exact(a)
        if a > eps and drop != f'pulse#{j}':
            new[tg] += a
    for m in M.mechs:
        if m.name == drop or not m.runs(t, u):
            continue
        c = m.value(s, u)
        if abs(c) > eps:
            new[m.target] += c
    return new

def trajectory(M, hist):
    s = {x: exact(v) for x, v in zip(M.sites, M.s0)}
    out = [dict(s)]
    for t, u in zip(M.beats(), hist):
        s = net(M, t, s, u)
        out.append(dict(s))
    return out

def poly_of(M, t, u):
    """ε = 0 时净增量是状态的多项式；返回单项式字典（用于剖面相等的精确核验）。"""
    P = {}
    def add(key, v):
        P[key] = P.get(key, Fr(0)) + v
    for tg, a in M.pulses_of(u):
        add((tg, ()), exact(a))
    for m in M.mechs:
        if not m.runs(t, u):
            continue
        g = m.factor(u)
        add((m.target, ()), m.b * g)
        if m.law == 'linear':
            for r in m.reads:
                add((m.target, (r,)), m.k * g)
        elif m.law == 'bilinear':
            add((m.target, tuple(sorted(m.reads))), m.k * g)
    return {k: v for k, v in P.items() if v != 0}

def same_profile(M1, M2, alphabet):
    """剖面相等：同位集、同初态、同窗，且每拍每输入的净增量多项式恒等（要求 ε = 0）。"""
    assert M1.eps == 0 and M2.eps == 0
    if M1.sites != M2.sites or M1.s0 != M2.s0 or (M1.t0, M1.t1) != (M2.t0, M2.t1):
        return False
    return all(poly_of(M1, t, u) == poly_of(M2, t, u) for t in M1.beats() for u in alphabet)

def hist_W(M, hist, seg, rest='rest'):
    """窗末读 Hist：实际史与以静置替换 seg 段的参照史，在 t1+1 比较界内全部状态（确定性，TV ∈ {0,1}）。"""
    ref = [rest if i in seg else u for i, u in enumerate(hist)]
    return trajectory(M, hist)[-1] != trajectory(M, ref)[-1]

def dep0(M, t, s, u, x, y, a, b):
    """基础依赖效应：置 y = a 与 y = b，x 的一步净变化是否不同。"""
    sa = dict(s); sa[y] = exact(a)
    sb = dict(s); sb[y] = exact(b)
    return (net(M, t, sa, u)[x] - sa[x]) != (net(M, t, sb, u)[x] - sb[x])

# ---------- Y2：机制级观测与辨识程序 ----------
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Decl:
    """申报及其认证状态。缺省一律未申报：未提供认证的前件不默认为真。
    proto：SEP、MOD、LOC、BEAT、INV-a（4.6 的原子判定协议）；inv_c、rec、ent、pos、ef4 见 4.2 与定理七；outf：申报 OUT-f 的过程名。"""
    proto: bool = False
    inv_c: bool = False
    rec: bool = False
    ent: bool = False
    pos: bool = False
    ef4: bool = False
    outf: frozenset = frozenset()

def full_decl(M, **kw):
    """全部前件申报齐备（线性律过程申报 OUT-f）；kw 可逐项撤去。"""
    d = dict(proto=True, inv_c=True, rec=True, ent=True, pos=True, ef4=True,
             outf=frozenset(m.name for m in M.mechs if m.law == 'linear'))
    d.update(kw)
    return Decl(**d)

def strip(decl, key):
    """撤去一项前件：PROTO、INV-c、REC、ENT、POS、EF4、OUT-f。"""
    m = {'PROTO': 'proto', 'INV-c': 'inv_c', 'REC': 'rec', 'ENT': 'ent', 'POS': 'pos', 'EF4': 'ef4'}
    if key == 'OUT-f':
        return Decl(**{**decl.__dict__, 'outf': frozenset()})
    return Decl(**{**decl.__dict__, m[key]: False})

@dataclass
class Lab:
    """宿载实验台。modular=False 时敲除触发补偿（违反 MOD）；side=(i, j) 时设定 i 连带改变 j（违反 LOC）；
    hidden 中的机制不出现在申报清单里（违反 INV-c）。"""
    M: MTM
    modular: bool = True
    compensate: dict = field(default_factory=dict)   # 敲除 m 时，由机制 m2 补回 m 的分项：{m: m2}
    side: tuple | None = None
    hidden: tuple = ()
    out_mode: dict = field(default_factory=dict)  # 过程名 → 'faithful'（OUT-f：读出忠实）／'const'（输出与读取解耦）；缺省：线性律 faithful，其余无读出
    pert: dict = field(default_factory=dict)       # 位 → 合法扰动 δ（缺省 +1）、'none'（无合法非零扰动）或 'unapplied'（存在而未实施）
    def inventory(self):
        return [(m.name, m.inside, m.target) for m in self.M.mechs if m.name not in self.hidden]
    def set_local(self, s, i, v):
        s2 = dict(s); s2[i] = exact(v)
        if self.side and self.side[0] == i:
            s2[self.side[1]] = s2[self.side[1]] + 1
        return s2
    def ko(self, t, s, u, name):
        new = net(self.M, t, s, u, drop=name)
        if not self.modular and name in self.compensate:
            m = next(mm for mm in self.M.mechs if mm.name == name)
            if m.runs(t, u):
                c = m.value(s, u)
                if abs(c) > self.M.eps:
                    new[m.target] += c         # 补偿机制恰好补回，敲除差为零
        return new
    def full(self, t, s, u):
        return net(self.M, t, s, u)
    def delta(self, i, s):
        """位 i 的合法扰动：返回 δ、'none' 或 'unapplied'。"""
        d = self.pert.get(i, Fr(1))
        return d
    def out(self, t, s, u, name):
        """过程读出。OUT-f（faithful）：读出是该过程实际读取位之值的组（常数律过程实际不读任何位，读出为空组）；
        const：读出恒为常值，与读取解耦；None：无读出。缺省与数理式 D15 一致：线性律 faithful，其余无读出。"""
        m = next(mm for mm in self.M.mechs if mm.name == name)
        if not m.runs(t, u):
            return None
        mode = self.out_mode.get(name, 'faithful' if m.law == 'linear' else None)
        if mode == 'faithful':
            return tuple(s[r] for r in m.reads)
        if mode == 'const':
            return ('c',)
        return None
    def faithful(self, name):
        m = next(mm for mm in self.M.mechs if mm.name == name)
        return self.out_mode.get(name, 'faithful' if m.law == 'linear' else None) == 'faithful'

# ---------- 三值判词与负判基础 ----------
# 判词值为 (v, B)：v ∈ {True, False, None}；v 为 False 时 B 是该 0 所消耗的前件集合。
# 合取为 0 时取各 0 合取项基础的交（撤去其中任一前件，每条 0 路径都失效）；析取为 0 时取并。
# v 为 None 时，第二分量是 ⊥ 的原因码集合（⊥_Prof、⊥_Pert、⊥_Obs、⊥_Sub、⊥_Inv、⊥_Rec、⊥_Id）。
V1 = (True, frozenset()); VU = (None, frozenset())
def V0(*b): return (False, frozenset(b))
def U(*codes): return (None, frozenset(codes))
def AND(*xs):
    zs = [b for v, b in xs if v is False]
    if zs: return (False, frozenset.intersection(*zs))
    if any(v is None for v, _ in xs): return (None, frozenset().union(*[b for v, b in xs if v is None]))
    return V1
def OR(*xs):
    if any(v is True for v, _ in xs): return V1
    if any(v is None for v, _ in xs): return (None, frozenset().union(*[b for v, b in xs if v is None]))
    return (False, frozenset().union(*[b for _, b in xs]))
def _tor(xs):
    xs = list(xs)
    return True if True in xs else (None if None in xs else False)
def _tand(*xs):
    if False in xs: return False
    if None in xs: return None
    return True

DOM = '⊥_Dom'
_LAWS = {'constant': 0, 'linear': 1, 'bilinear': 2}
def validate_mechanism_decl(lab, hist, decl, T=None):
    """机制级申报的统一入口（数理式 D20、本式 3.5、4.2）：返回 None（通过）或 (DOM, 原因)。
    不合法的申报不进入求值；未提交的认证不在此处报错，而由 decl 缺项在判词中给出 ⊥。"""
    M = lab.M
    def bad(why): return (DOM, why)
    num = lambda v: isinstance(v, (int, Fr)) and not isinstance(v, bool)
    if not M.sites or len(set(M.sites)) != len(M.sites) or not all(isinstance(x, str) and x for x in M.sites): return bad('位名为空或重复')
    if len(M.s0) != len(M.sites) or not all(num(v) and v >= 0 for v in M.s0): return bad('起点状态与位集不符或含负值')
    if not (num(M.eps) and M.eps >= 0): return bad('ε 不是非负有理数')
    if not (isinstance(M.t0, int) and isinstance(M.t1, int) and 1 <= M.t0 <= M.t1): return bad('申报期 t₀、t₁ 不合法')
    TT = tuple(T if T is not None else M.T)
    if not TT or len(set(TT)) != len(TT) or not set(TT) <= set(M.sites): return bad('申报 T 为空、重复或不在位集内')
    names = [m.name for m in M.mechs]
    if len(set(names)) != len(names): return bad('过程名重复')
    win = set(M.beats())
    for m in M.mechs:
        if m.target not in M.sites: return bad(f'过程 {m.name} 的作用对象缺失或不在位集内')
        if m.law not in _LAWS: return bad(f'过程 {m.name} 的律不属 F 类')
        if not set(m.reads) <= set(M.sites): return bad(f'过程 {m.name} 的读取位不在位集内')
        if m.law == 'bilinear' and len(m.reads) != 2: return bad(f'过程 {m.name} 的双线性律须恰读两位')
        if m.law == 'linear' and not m.reads: return bad(f'过程 {m.name} 的线性律读取位为空')
        if m.law == 'constant' and m.reads: return bad(f'过程 {m.name} 的常数律不得有读取位')
        if not (num(m.k) and num(m.b)): return bad(f'过程 {m.name} 的参数不是有理数')
        if m.beats is not None and not set(m.beats) <= win: return bad(f'过程 {m.name} 的运行拍越出申报窗')
    for u, ps in M.pulses:
        for tg, a in ps:
            if tg not in M.sites or not (num(a) and a >= 0): return bad(f'输入 {u} 的脉冲对象或幅度不合法')
    if len(hist) != len(M.beats()): return bad('输入史长度与拍数不符')
    for i, d in lab.pert.items():
        if i not in M.sites: return bad(f'扰动位 {i} 不在位集内')
        if not (d in ('none', 'unapplied') or (num(d) and d != 0)): return bad(f'位 {i} 的扰动不是合法非零值或申报状态')
    if not isinstance(decl, Decl) or not set(decl.outf) <= set(names): return bad('申报格式不合法或 OUT-f 指向不存在的过程')
    # 实际轨迹逐拍精确生成，任一位为负即越出 F 定义域（数理式 4.2、D20；不截零、不设容差），停止求值
    st = {x: exact(v) for x, v in zip(M.sites, M.s0)}
    for t, u in zip(M.beats(), hist):
        st = net(M, t, st, u)
        for x in M.sites:
            if st[x] < 0: return bad(f'拍 {t} 后位 {x} 的深度为 {st[x]}，负值越出 F 定义域')
    return None
def identify(lab, hist, decl, T=None, detail=False):
    """由 Y2 观测与申报 decl 构造数理式 F 原子（三值，含负判基础），再按 D19 求 Trace、Part、HDyn、Q。
    三层分立：观测事实（轨迹、敲除差、局部响应、读出）；申报及其认证状态（decl）；判词（值与所消耗的前件）。
    规则（4.6 节原子构造规则）：
      Act(m)：|Δ_m| > ε 为 1，否则 0（前件 PROTO）；
      HistCond(m)：已认证可用位上有局部响应为 1（LOC 排他：证据边 (i,t)→m 记 1）；
                   m 有效、一切候选位都实施了合法扰动且无响应为 0（前件 PROTO，及被排除位的形成否定前件）；
                   响应只在可用性未定的位上、或有候选位未实施或无合法扰动（扰动覆盖不足），为 ⊥；m 无效且无响应为 ⊥；
      Cond(m)：同上；无效过程在申报 OUT-f 时以读出响应判定，否则 ⊥_Sub；
      Form、Mark：见到即 1；未见到时须 PROTO、INV-c、REC，记录起点已大于 ε 的位另须 ENT，才判 0，否则 ⊥；
      Part、HDyn 的 0 另须 INV-c；Ident、HistEv 由 POS、REC 给出。
    pert（Lab）：每位所施的扰动；'none'（已申报无合法非零扰动）判 ⊥_Pert，'unapplied' 与所施候选不合法判 ⊥_Obs，都不能支撑负判。"""
    err = validate_mechanism_decl(lab, hist, decl, T)
    if err is not None:                        # 不合法申报不进入求值：各判词 ⊥，并报告原因码
        res = {'trace': None, 'part': None, 'hdyn': None, 'Q': None, 'unexplained': None, 'dom': err,
               'basis': {'trace': frozenset(), 'part': frozenset(), 'hdyn': frozenset(), 'Q': frozenset()}}
        if detail: res.update(rows=[], edges=[], form={}, mark={})
        return res
    M = lab.M; eps = M.eps; T = tuple(T if T is not None else M.T)
    traj = trajectory(M, hist)
    inv = lab.inventory()
    pre = {x for x in M.sites if traj[0][x] > eps}
    form = {x: [] for x in M.sites}; marks = {x: [] for x in M.sites}
    def form_neg_basis(i):
        """位 i 的形成否定是否已认证；已认证返回其基础，否则 None。"""
        if not (decl.proto and decl.inv_c and decl.rec): return None
        if i in pre and not decl.ent: return None
        return frozenset({'PROTO', 'INV-c', 'REC'} | ({'ENT'} if i in pre else set()))
    def form_unk_code(i):
        """形成否定未认证的原因码：缺 PROTO 为 ⊥_Prof，缺 INV-c 为 ⊥_Inv，缺 REC 或（起点已有的位）缺 ENT 为 ⊥_Rec。"""
        c = set()
        if not decl.proto: c.add('⊥_Prof')
        if not decl.inv_c: c.add('⊥_Inv')
        if not decl.rec or (i in pre and not decl.ent): c.add('⊥_Rec')
        return frozenset(c)
    def pert_code(i, s):
        d = lab.delta(i, s)
        if d == 'none': return '⊥_Pert'                   # 已申报 D_i(s) 为空：无合法非零扰动
        return '⊥_Obs'                                    # 存在而未实施；或所施候选不合法（设定值或一步后继为负）——合法响应未取得，不推 D_i(s) 为空
    parts = []; hd = []; unexplained = False; rows = []; edges = []
    for idx, (t, u) in enumerate(zip(M.beats(), hist)):
        s = traj[idx]; full = lab.full(t, s, u)
        explained = {x: Fr(0) for x in M.sites}
        if decl.proto:
            for x in M.sites:                                   # 越阈即形成（F 类入账规则）
                if s[x] <= eps < traj[idx + 1][x]:
                    form[x].append(t)
        for j, (tg, _a) in enumerate(M.pulses_of(u)):       # 脉冲逐个敲除
            c = full[tg] - net(M, t, s, u, drop=f'pulse#{j}')[tg]
            explained[tg] += c
            if c > eps and decl.proto:
                form[tg].append(t); marks[tg].append(t)
        for name, inside, tg in inv:
            c = full[tg] - lab.ko(t, s, u, name)[tg]       # 敲除差 = 截阈入账项 ĉ_m
            explained[tg] += c
            act = abs(c) > eps
            if decl.proto and act and c > eps and s[tg] <= eps:
                form[tg].append(t)
            if decl.proto and act and not inside:
                marks[tg].append(t)
            if t < M.t0:
                continue
            if not decl.proto:
                UP = U('⊥_Prof')                            # 未申报原子判定协议：只有状态级证据
                rows.append((name, t, inside, tg, c, UP, UP, UP)); parts.append(UP) if inside else None; hd.append(UP)
                continue
            vact = V1 if act else V0('PROTO')
            cand = [i for i in T if s[i] > eps]
            known, unk, excl = [], [], {}
            for i in cand:
                if any(f < t for f in form[i]): known.append(i)
                else:
                    b = form_neg_basis(i)
                    if b is None: unk.append(i)
                    else: excl[i] = b
            def response(i, fn):
                d = lab.delta(i, s)
                if d in ('none', 'unapplied'): return None
                if s[i] + d < 0: return None
                s2 = lab.set_local(s, i, s[i] + d)
                if any(v < 0 for v in lab.full(t, s2, u).values()): return None   # 设定后的一步后继越出 F 定义域：不是合法扰动
                return fn(s2)
            ko_val = lambda s2: lab.full(t, s2, u)[tg] - lab.ko(t, s2, u, name)[tg]
            resp = {i: (lambda r: None if r is None else r != c)(response(i, ko_val)) for i in cand}
            rk = [i for i in known if resp[i] is True]
            if rk:
                vhc = V1
                edges += [(f'{i}@{t}', f'{name}@{t}') for i in rk]
            elif not cand:
                vhc = V0('PROTO')
            elif act:
                if any(resp[i] is True for i in unk) or any(resp[i] is None for i in known + unk):
                    vhc = (None, frozenset().union(*[form_unk_code(i) for i in unk if resp[i] is True],
                                                   [pert_code(i, s) for i in known + unk if resp[i] is None]))
                else:
                    b = {'PROTO'}
                    for i, bx in excl.items():
                        if resp[i] is not False: b |= bx       # 被排除位若有响应或未扰动，0 依赖其形成否定
                    vhc = (False, frozenset(b))
            else:
                vhc = U('⊥_Sub')                               # 无效过程且无响应
            o = lab.out(t, s, u, name)
            running = _runs(lab, t, u, name)
            if not running:
                vcond = V0('PROTO')
            elif not cand:
                vcond = V0('PROTO')
            elif act:
                vcond = vhc
            elif rk:
                vcond = V1
            elif o is not None and name in decl.outf:
                orsp = {i: (lambda r: None if r is None else r != o)(response(i, lambda s2: lab.out(t, s2, u, name))) for i in cand}
                if any(orsp[i] is True for i in known):
                    vcond = V1
                elif any(orsp[i] is True for i in unk) or any(orsp[i] is None for i in known + unk):
                    vcond = (None, frozenset().union(*[form_unk_code(i) for i in unk if orsp[i] is True],
                                                     [pert_code(i, s) for i in known + unk if orsp[i] is None]))
                else:
                    b = {'PROTO', 'OUT-f'}
                    for i, bx in excl.items():
                        if orsp[i] is not False: b |= bx
                    vcond = (False, frozenset(b))
            else:
                vcond = U('⊥_Sub')
            if inside:
                parts.append(vcond)
            hd.append(AND(V1 if tg in T else V0('PROTO'), vact, vhc))
            rows.append((name, t, inside, tg, c, vact, vcond, vhc))
        for x in M.sites:
            if full[x] - s[x] != explained[x]:
                unexplained = True
    end = traj[-1]
    def found(lst, x):
        if lst: return V1
        b = form_neg_basis(x)
        return (None, form_unk_code(x)) if b is None else (False, b)
    elig = AND(*[AND(V1 if end[i] > eps else V0(), found(form[i], i)) for i in T])
    src = OR(*[found(marks[i], i) for i in T])
    vtrace = AND(src, elig, V1 if decl.pos else U('⊥_Id'), V1 if decl.rec else U('⊥_Rec'))
    def closed_or(xs):
        v = OR(*xs) if xs else (False, frozenset())
        if v[0] is False:
            return (False, v[1] | {'INV-c'}) if decl.inv_c else U('⊥_Inv')
        return v
    vpart = closed_or(parts); vhd = closed_or(hd)
    vq = AND(vtrace, vpart, vhd)
    config = {'T': T, 'ε': M.eps, '申报期': (M.t0, M.t1), '输入史': tuple(hist),
              '扰动设定': tuple(sorted((i, str(d)) for i, d in lab.pert.items())) or '各位缺省 +1',
              '实验台': ('MOD' if lab.modular else '敲除触发补偿') + ('' if lab.side is None else '；设定连带') + ('' if not lab.hidden else '；隐去过程')}
    res = {'trace': vtrace[0], 'part': vpart[0], 'hdyn': vhd[0], 'Q': vq[0], 'unexplained': unexplained, 'dom': None, 'config': config,
           'basis': {'trace': vtrace[1], 'part': vpart[1], 'hdyn': vhd[1], 'Q': vq[1]}}
    if detail:
        res['rows'] = rows; res['edges'] = edges
        res['form'] = {i: found(form[i], i) for i in T}; res['mark'] = {i: found(marks[i], i) for i in T}
    return res

# ---------- 4.1 五元组报告适配器 ----------
from collections import namedtuple
Det = namedtuple('Det', 'v Code Level Dom Read')
_DECL_NAMES = (('proto', 'SEP、MOD、LOC、BEAT、INV-a'), ('inv_c', 'INV-c'), ('rec', 'REC'), ('ent', 'ENT'), ('pos', 'POS'), ('ef4', 'EF4'))
def report(res, decl, key, read=None):
    """把 identify 的一项判词写成 4.1 的五元组 Det(v, Code, Level, Dom, Read)。
    v ∈ {1, 0, '⊥'}；Code：1 为「见证」，0 为「0：前件 …」（前件清单 B），⊥ 为原因码；
    Level 为 Y₂；Dom 为所申报的协议与前件，以及实际生效的配置（T、ε、申报期、输入史、扰动设定、实验台）；Read 为读数（缺省给出证据边与未解释残差标志）。
    申报不合法时 v = '⊥'，Code 为 ⊥_Dom 及原因。"""
    dom = {'申报的协议与前件': tuple(n for a, n in _DECL_NAMES if getattr(decl, a)) + ((('OUT-f：' + '、'.join(sorted(decl.outf))),) if decl.outf else ()),
           '实际生效的配置': res.get('config')}
    if res.get('dom'):
        return Det('⊥', res['dom'][0] + '：' + res['dom'][1], 'Y₂', dom, read)
    v, b = res[key], res['basis'][key]
    if v is True: val, code = 1, '见证'
    elif v is False: val, code = 0, '0：前件 ' + '、'.join(sorted(b))
    else: val, code = '⊥', '、'.join(sorted(b)) or '⊥'
    if read is None:
        read = {'未解释残差': res['unexplained']}
        if 'edges' in res: read['证据边'] = tuple(res['edges'])
    return Det(val, code, 'Y₂', dom, read)
def report_value(res, decl, kind=None, key=None):
    """单个原子的五元组，与复合报告同源：从同一次 identify 的结果 res（须 detail=True）中按种类与键取原子，
    Dom 携带该次判定的实际配置，Read 记来源上下文（种类、位或过程名与拍号）。
    kind ∈ {'Form', 'Mark'}：key 为位；kind ∈ {'Act', 'Cond', 'HistCond'}：key 为 (过程名, 拍)；kind = 'Selfloop'：界内自反馈型。
    res 不含所求原子时判 ⊥_Undef，不从别的实例补填配置。
    只给单个 (v, B) 而无判定结果（旧调用形式）时，没有来源上下文，判 ⊥_Undef，不输出空配置冒充完整报告。"""
    if isinstance(res, tuple) or kind is None:
        return Det('⊥', '⊥_Undef：缺来源上下文（须给出同一次 identify 的结果、原子种类与键）', 'Y₂',
                   {'申报的协议与前件': None, '实际生效的配置': '缺来源上下文'}, None)
    if res.get('dom'):
        return report(res, decl, 'Q', read={'种类': kind, '对象': key})
    if kind in ('Form', 'Mark'):
        vb = res.get(kind.lower(), {}).get(key)
    elif kind in ('Act', 'Cond', 'HistCond'):
        col = {'Act': 5, 'Cond': 6, 'HistCond': 7}[kind]
        vb = next((r[col] for r in res.get('rows', []) if (r[0], r[1]) == key), None)
    elif kind == 'Selfloop':
        vb = selfloop_noext(res, decl) if 'rows' in res else None
    else:
        vb = None
    read = {'种类': kind, '对象': key}
    if vb is None:
        return Det('⊥', '⊥_Undef：所给判定结果中无此原子', 'Y₂', {'申报的协议与前件': None, '实际生效的配置': res.get('config')}, read)
    return report({'x': vb[0], 'basis': {'x': vb[1]}, 'unexplained': res.get('unexplained'), 'config': res.get('config')}, decl, 'x', read)
def selfloop_noext(res, decl):
    """4.8 节界内自反馈型判词的受限实现：只适用于无界外分量、无界外通路的模型（本模型类即如此），
    此时外钳 LOC 与 LOC 相同。带界外通路的实例不用本函数，判 ⊥ 或另行实施外钳干预。
    1：B 内有过程 Act = HistCond = 1；0：INV-c 与 EF4 下，B 内每一运行过程的 Act 或 HistCond 已判为 0；⊥：其余。
    返回 (v, 基础)。"""
    rows = [r for r in res['rows'] if r[2]]
    v = OR(*[AND(r[5], r[7]) for r in rows]) if rows else (False, frozenset())
    if v[0] is False:
        if decl.inv_c and decl.ef4:
            return (False, v[1] | {'INV-c', 'EF4'})
        return U(*(['⊥_Inv'] if not decl.inv_c else []) + (['⊥_Undef'] if not decl.ef4 else []))
    return v

def formation(M, hist):
    """形成记录（与数理式 D16 及 field_final 同一规则）：有效脉冲；或有效机制分项使该位越过 ε。"""
    eps = M.eps; traj = trajectory(M, hist); form = {x: [] for x in M.sites}
    for idx, (t, u) in enumerate(zip(M.beats(), hist)):
        s = traj[idx]
        for tg, a in M.pulses_of(u):
            if exact(a) > eps:
                form[tg].append(t)
        for m in M.mechs:
            if m.runs(t, u):
                c = m.value(s, u)
                if abs(c) > eps and c > eps and s[m.target] <= eps:
                    form[m.target].append(t)
    return form

def t_max(M, hist):
    end = trajectory(M, hist)[-1]; form = formation(M, hist)
    return tuple(x for x in M.sites if end[x] > M.eps and form[x])

def _runs(lab, t, u, name):
    m = next(mm for mm in lab.M.mechs if mm.name == name)
    return m.runs(t, u)
~~~

A.5　experiments.py　第二层判据的核验 C1—C17（定理五至七，引理 6.1、八至十二，4.2 申报入口，4.6，4.8，4.9 报告）

~~~python
"""沉积场判定式 附录 A.5：第二层判据的核验 C1—C15。运行：python3 experiments.py（mtm.py、field_final.py 置同一目录）"""
import sys, os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from mtm import Mech, MTM, Lab, truth, trajectory, same_profile, hist_W, dep0, identify, net, full_decl, strip, selfloop_noext, Decl, report, report_value
def sl(res, decl):
    v = selfloop_noext(res, decl)[0]
    return {True: 1, False: 0, None: None}[v]
import itertools, random
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import sys
import os as _os
sys.path.insert(0, _os.path.dirname(_os.path.abspath(__file__)))   # field_final.py 与本文件同目录（与《场具化数理式》v1.0 附录 A.1 逐字相同）
from field_final import run
H = Fr(1, 2)
ok_all = True
def check(cond, msg):
    global ok_all
    print(('  通过  ' if cond else '  失败  ') + msg)
    ok_all = ok_all and cond
def all_histories(M, alphabet):
    return itertools.product(alphabet, repeat=len(M.beats()))
def level1_equal(M1, M2, alphabet):
    """Y1 全族：一切输入史的轨迹相同，且抽样状态上的基础依赖效应相同。
       两模型在共同冻结的 C_op 下比较：同一 ε、字母表、史集（W 内一切输入史）、抽样状态与设定值 (0, 1)。"""
    for h in all_histories(M1, alphabet):
        if trajectory(M1, list(h)) != trajectory(M2, list(h)):
            return False
    for t in M1.beats():
        for u in alphabet:
            for vals in itertools.product([Fr(0), H, Fr(1), Fr(2)], repeat=len(M1.sites)):
                s = dict(zip(M1.sites, vals))
                if net(M1, t, s, u) != net(M2, t, s, u):
                    return False
                for x in M1.sites:
                    for y in M1.sites:
                        if dep0(M1, t, s, u, x, y, 0, 1) != dep0(M2, t, s, u, x, y, 0, 1):
                            return False
    return True
# ---------------- C1 剖面等价而职责相异（定理五（一）） ----------------
print('C1　剖面等价而职责相异')
scan_in = Mech('scan', 'x', True, 'linear', ('x',), Fr(0), Fr(0), (), (1,))
scan_out = Mech('scan', 'x', False, 'linear', ('x',), Fr(0), Fr(0), (), (1,))
base = dict(sites=('x',), s0=(0,), T=('x',), eps=Fr(0), t0=1, t1=1, pulses=(('w', (('x', 1),)),))
alph = ('rest', 'w')
# (a) 作用：+a 与 −a 相抵 —— Act/HDyn 不同
A1 = MTM(**base, mechs=(scan_in,))
B1 = MTM(**base, mechs=(scan_in, Mech('m1', 'x', True, 'linear', ('x',), H, Fr(0), (), (1,)),
                        Mech('m2', 'x', True, 'linear', ('x',), -H, Fr(0), (), (1,))))
h = ['w', 'rest']
check(same_profile(A1, B1, alph) and level1_equal(A1, B1, alph), '(a) 相抵对：剖面恒等，共同 C_op 下 Y1 全族读数相同')
check(truth(A1, h)['hdyn'] is False and truth(B1, h)['hdyn'] is True, '(a) 相抵对：HDyn 一为 0、一为 1')
# (b) 出自迹应：常数律写入 与 依迹应写入＋依迹应抵偿 —— HistCond/HDyn 不同
A2 = MTM(**base, mechs=(scan_in, Mech('c', 'x', True, 'constant', (), Fr(0), H, (), (1,))))
B2 = MTM(**base, mechs=(scan_in, Mech('m1', 'x', True, 'linear', ('x',), H, Fr(0), (), (1,)),
                        Mech('m2', 'x', True, 'linear', ('x',), -H, H, (), (1,))))
check(same_profile(A2, B2, alph) and level1_equal(A2, B2, alph), '(b) 来源对：剖面恒等，共同 C_op 下 Y1 全族读数相同')
check(truth(A2, h)['hdyn'] is False and truth(B2, h)['hdyn'] is True, '(b) 来源对：HDyn 一为 0、一为 1')
# (c) 信号留迹：界外放大（+x/2，信号在 x 上留下有效改变）对 门控停退（信号只停下退浅机制）—— r 不同
seed = Mech('seed', 'x', True, 'constant', (), Fr(0), Fr(1), (), (0,))
decay_amp = Mech('decay', 'x', True, 'linear', ('x',), -H, Fr(0))
amp = Mech('amp', 'x', False, 'linear', ('x',), H, Fr(0), (('rest', 0),))
decay_gate = Mech('decay', 'x', True, 'linear', ('x',), -H, Fr(0), (('a', 0),))
reset_g = Mech('reset', 'g', True, 'linear', ('g',), Fr(-1), Fr(0))
cbase = dict(sites=('x', 'g'), s0=(0, 0), T=('x',), eps=Fr(0), t0=1, t1=2, pulses=(('a', (('g', 1),)),))
A3 = MTM(**cbase, mechs=(seed, decay_amp, amp, reset_g))
B3 = MTM(**cbase, mechs=(seed, decay_gate, reset_g))
h3 = ['rest', 'a', 'rest']
alph3 = ('rest', 'a')
check(same_profile(A3, B3, alph3) and level1_equal(A3, B3, alph3), '(c) 留迹对：剖面恒等，共同 C_op 下 Y1 全族读数相同')
check(hist_W(A3, h3, {1}) and hist_W(B3, h3, {1}), '(c) 留迹对：窗末读 Hist 两者都为 1')
check(truth(A3, h3)['trace'] is True and truth(B3, h3)['trace'] is False, '(c) 留迹对：TraceSet（r）一为 1、一为 0')
# (d) 参与：读出过程载体在 B 内 对 在界外 —— Part 不同
ext_w = Mech('ew', 'x', False, 'linear', ('x',), H, Fr(0), (), (1,))
A4 = MTM(**base, mechs=(scan_in, ext_w))
B4 = MTM(**base, mechs=(scan_out, ext_w))
check(same_profile(A4, B4, alph) and level1_equal(A4, B4, alph), '(d) 载体对：剖面恒等，共同 C_op 下 Y1 全族读数相同')
check(truth(A4, h)['part'] is True and truth(B4, h)['part'] is False, '(d) 载体对：Part 一为 1、一为 0')
# (f) 形成记录：初值已在 ε 之上的位上，信号脉冲 对 输入门控的界内常数律 —— q（Form）不同
fbase = dict(sites=('x',), s0=(1,), T=('x',), eps=Fr(0), t0=1, t1=1)
A5 = MTM(**fbase, pulses=(('w', (('x', 1),)),), mechs=(scan_in,))
B5 = MTM(**fbase, pulses=(), mechs=(scan_in, Mech('g', 'x', True, 'constant', (), Fr(0), Fr(1), (('rest', 0),)),
                                     Mech('e+', 'x', False, 'constant', (), Fr(0), Fr(1), (), (1,)),
                                     Mech('i-', 'x', True, 'constant', (), Fr(0), Fr(-1), (), (1,))))
check(same_profile(A5, B5, alph) and level1_equal(A5, B5, alph), '(f) 形成对：剖面恒等，共同 C_op 下 Y1 全族读数相同')
from mtm import formation as _fm
check(truth(A5, h)['trace'] is True and truth(B5, h)['trace'] is False and not _fm(B5, h)['x'],
      '(f) 形成对（两者 r 均成立）：记录内恒 > ε 时 q 的形成记录一为非空、一为空，TraceSet 一为 1、一为 0')
# (e) 由最大迹应集看 Fld：四对中每一对 ∃T 的结论也不同
def fld(M, hist):
    sites = M.sites; best = False
    for r in range(1, len(sites) + 1):
        for T in itertools.combinations(sites, r):
            q = truth(M, hist, T)['Q']
            if q is True: return True
            if q is None: best = None
    return best
check(all(fld(A, hh) != fld(B, hh) for A, B, hh in [(A1, B1, h), (A2, B2, h), (A3, B3, h3), (A4, B4, h)]),
      '(e) 四对的 ∃T Q 都不同：Fld 亦不能由 Y1 判定')
# ---------------- C2 机制级辨识（定理六）随机回归 ----------------
print('C2　机制级辨识：SEP、MOD、LOC、BEAT、INV-a 下重建 F 原子')
rng = random.Random(20261029)
def rand_mtm(rng):
    n = rng.randint(1, 3); sites = tuple('xyz'[:n])
    T = tuple(sorted(rng.sample(sites, rng.randint(1, n))))
    eps = rng.choice([Fr(0), Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 2)])
    alphabet = ('rest', 'a', 'b')
    pulses = tuple((u, tuple((rng.choice(sites), rng.choice([Fr(0), Fr(1, 4), Fr(1), Fr(2)]))
                             for _ in range(rng.randint(0, 2)))) for u in ('a', 'b'))
    mechs = []
    for i in range(rng.randint(1, 5)):
        law = rng.choice(['constant', 'linear', 'bilinear'])
        if law == 'constant': reads = ()
        elif law == 'linear': reads = tuple(rng.sample(sites, rng.randint(1, n)))
        else: reads = (rng.choice(sites), rng.choice(sites))
        k = Fr(rng.choice([-1, 0, 1, 2]), rng.choice([1, 2, 4]))
        b = Fr(rng.choice([-1, 0, 0, 1, 2]), rng.choice([1, 2, 4]))
        if law == 'constant' and rng.random() < 0.3: b = Fr(0)
        gate = tuple((u, rng.choice([0, 1, 1, 2])) for u in alphabet if rng.random() < 0.4)
        beats = None if rng.random() < 0.6 else tuple(sorted(rng.sample(range(0, 3), rng.randint(1, 3))))
        mechs.append(Mech(f'm{i}', rng.choice(sites), rng.random() < 0.7, law, reads, k, b, gate, beats))
    M = MTM(sites, tuple(rng.choice([0, 0, 1]) for _ in sites), T, eps, 1, 2, pulses, tuple(mechs))
    hist = [rng.choice(alphabet) for _ in M.beats()]
    return M, hist
n_ok = n_tot = 0; n_pos = 0; n_und = 0; n_pert = 0
while n_tot < 600:
    M, hist = rand_mtm(rng)
    try:
        tr = truth(M, hist)
    except ValueError:
        continue          # 越出 F 定义域（负深度等）
    idr = identify(Lab(M), hist, full_decl(M))
    n_tot += 1
    def leq(a, b):   # 信息序：a ⪯ b
        return a is None or a == b
    sound = all(leq(idr[k], tr[k]) for k in ('trace', 'part', 'hdyn', 'Q')) and not idr['unexplained']
    pert_only = idr['hdyn'] is None and idr['basis']['hdyn'] == frozenset({'⊥_Obs'})    # 所施缺省设定的后继越出 F：合法响应未取得
    n_pert += pert_only
    exact_hd = (idr['hdyn'] == tr['hdyn'] or pert_only) and idr['trace'] == tr['trace']
    n_ok += sound and exact_hd; n_pos += (tr['Q'] is True)
    n_und += (idr['Q'] is None)
    if not (sound and exact_hd):
        print('   反例', M, hist, tr, idr); break
check(n_ok == n_tot, f'随机 {n_tot} 例：重建的 Trace 与上游求值器逐项相等，HDyn 相等或因所施缺省设定不合法而判 ⊥_Obs（{n_pert} 例：缺省 +1 设定使一步后继越出 F 定义域，合法响应未取得）；Part、Q 在信息序下不越真值（Q 为 ⊥ 者 {n_und} 例：该拍无有效分项、又无读取输出的常数律或双线性律过程，其读取依赖在分辨率下不可见，或合法响应未取得）；Q = 1 者 {n_pos} 例；闭合核对全部无残差')
~~~
